Using this framework, we benchmark several families of open-weight VLMs, jointly evaluating face verification accuracy and explanation quality. Our results highlight remaining shortcomings of produced explanations, and emphasize the need for such explanation quality metrics to get a complete picture of model performance. The proposed benchmark and open-source evaluation harness provide a foundation for proper benchmarking and future fine-tuning of explainable face recognition systems.

</details>


### [68] [Catena: A Comprehensive Software Suite for Large-Scale Connectomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21887)
*Samia Mohinta,Pedro Gómez-Gálvez,Shi Yan Lee,Daniel Franco-Barranco,Michael Clayton,Stephan Preibisch,Jan Funke,Albert Cardona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Catena 是一个开源、面向开发者、模块化且容器化的连接组学软件套件，集成神经元/细胞器分割、突触检测、微管追踪和神经递质推断，旨在以可复现、可扩展的方式从电镜体数据重建连接组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电镜体数据是连接组图谱的金标准，但神经元重建、校对和突触注释通常依赖多个碎片化、维护不一致或专有软件工具，阻碍了可复现性与自动化。

Method: 提出 Catena 开源软件套件，将三维神经元与细胞器分割、突触检测、微管追踪、神经递质推断等模块组织为可组合、分块式处理流水线；采用预训练机器学习模型降低算力与标注数据需求并支持微调；模块完全容器化，封装依赖以保证工作站与集群上的一致执行。

Result: 通过整合开放组件、可共享模型和容器化运行时，Catena 为从电镜体数据映射细胞连接组提供了可复现且可扩展的解决方案。摘要未给出定量性能指标。

Conclusion: Catena 以模块化、可扩展、容器化和预训练模型为核心，缓解连接组学工具链碎片化与可复现性问题；代码与文档见 https://github.com/Mohinta2892/catena.git。

Abstract: The gold standard datasets for mapping connectomes are electron microscopy volumes of densely labeled neural tissue at nanometer resolution. Yet reconstructing and proofreading neuronal arbors and annotating all synapses requires pipelining multiple software tools that are often fragmented, inconsistently maintained, or proprietary, hindering reproducibility and automation. Here, we introduce Catena, an open-source, comprehensive, developer-centric software suite for connectomics that integrates modules for 3D neuron and organelle segmentation, synapse detection, microtubule tracking, and neurotransmitter inference. Catena organizes its modules in composable, chunk-wise processing pipelines in a completely documented, extensible, and adaptable design. We further reduce compute and ground-truth data requirements with pretrained machine learning models, facilitating fine-tuning. Catena ships fully containerized modules that encapsulate evolving dependencies for consistent execution across workstations and clusters. By consolidating open components, shareable models, and containerized runtimes, Catena delivers a reproducible and scalable approach to mapping cellular connectomes from electron microscopy volumes. Code and documentation: https://github.com/Mohinta2892/catena.git

</details>


### [69] [The Role of Radiometric Features in Cross-Site Leaf-Wood Segmentation of LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21903)
*Roman Kaharlytskyi,Derek T. Robinson,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文挑战“为跨传感器兼容而排除辐射特征”的既有设计，通过 Heidelberg TLS（1550 nm）训练、Ontario RPA-LS（905 nm）测试的跨站点/跨平台实验表明：仅用几何特征（含SOTA深度学习模型）难以泛化到稀疏俯视航测点云，F1≤0.56；引入强度、回波次数等辐射特征后F1提升至0.61，木材召回率由0.16增至0.35（+119%），并改善枝干结构连通性，更利于QSM重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单木叶木分割是定量结构模型（QSM）和非破坏性生物量估计的关键步骤。现有方法通常排除强度、回波次数等辐射特征，以追求跨传感器兼容性；作者质疑这一设计选择，并系统评估跨站点、跨平台的泛化能力。

Method: 采用跨域实验设计：在公开 Heidelberg 数据集（地面 TLS，1550 nm）上训练，在加拿大安大略省新数据集（RPA-LS，905 nm）上测试。对比几何-only方法（包括在高密度LiDAR数据上训练的SOTA深度学习模型）与加入辐射特征（intensity、return number、number of returns）的方法，并评估F1、木材召回率及枝干结构连通性/碎片化程度。

Result: 几何-only方法难以泛化到稀疏、俯视的航测点云，F1≤0.56；加入辐射特征后F1提升至0.61，木材召回率从0.16提升到0.35（相对提升119%）。几何-only方法常导致树干和枝条组件碎片化；辐射特征有助于保持结构连通性，生成更连贯的树体结构，更适合QSM重建。

Conclusion: 几何模式具有视角依赖性，容易过拟合特定扫描模式；辐射特征编码了物理材质属性，可在不同传感器和环境中泛化。因此，叶木分割不应为跨传感器兼容而简单排除辐射特征，纳入辐射信息可显著提升跨平台泛化性和QSM重建质量。

Abstract: Leaf-wood segmentation of individual trees from LiDAR point clouds is essential for quantitative structure models (QSMs) used in non-destructive biomass estimation. Existing segmentation methods typically exclude radiometric features (e.g., intensity, return number) to maximize cross-sensor compatibility. We challenge this design choice by evaluating cross-site and cross-platform generalization: training on the public Heidelberg dataset (terrestrial TLS, 1550nm) and testing on a novel dataset from Ontario, Canada (RPA-LS, 905nm). Results show that geometry-only methods - including state-of-the-art deep learning models trained on high-density LiDAR datasets - fail to generalize to the sparse, top-down geometry of aerial scans, achieving F1 scores <= 0.56. Incorporating radiometric features (intensity, return number, number of returns) improves F1 to 0.61, but more critically, increases wood recall by 119% from 0.16 to 0.35. Furthermore, geometry-only approaches often result in fragmented stem and branch components. We find that leveraging radiometric features preserves greater structural connectivity, resulting in more coherent architectures that are better suited for QSM reconstruction. We demonstrate that while geometric patterns are view-dependent and prone to overfitting scan patterns, radiometric features encode physical material properties that generalize across disparate sensors and environments.

</details>


### [70] [Info3R: Information-Adaptive Test-Time Training for 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21938)
*Sunghyun Baek,Hanna Bae,Minchan Kwon,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Info3R：面向在线3D重建的信息自适应测试时训练方法，通过信息感知的状态更新与动态状态重置，缓解长序列中的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的在线3D重建方法在处理长图像流时，忽略了两类关键信号：(1) 每一新到帧的重要性；(2) 模型内部状态的信息饱和程度。这导致长序列中性能明显下降，难以支撑真实场景的在线部署。

Method: 提出Info3R，一种信息自适应的测试时训练方法。核心包含两部分：(1) 信息感知的状态更新——根据每帧的冗余度与信息量来调节状态更新的强度；(2) 动态状态重置——当状态更新的累计幅度与模型预测置信度触发条件时执行重置，以恢复状态的可塑性（接纳新观测的能力），并辅以锚点-世界坐标对齐（anchor-to-world alignment）。

Result: 在相机位姿估计、视频深度估计与3D重建任务上取得一致提升，并显著缓解长序列评测中的性能退化。在KITTI Odometry上，平均ATE较LongStream降低1.68倍，体现其在长距离户外序列上的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模帧重要性与状态饱和信号，Info3R有效平衡了状态的稳定性与可塑性，为长序列在线3D重建提供了更鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently achieved strong performance on 3D reconstruction from images, and recent works extend them to process video streams in an online manner for real-world deployment. However, existing methods overlook two key signals when handling long image streams: the importance of each incoming frame and the information saturation of the model's internal state. In this paper, we propose Info3R, a novel information-adaptive test-time training method for the online 3D reconstruction. We introduce an information-aware state update that modulates the state update strength based on the redundancy and informativeness of each incoming frame. To restore the state's plasticity -- its capacity to incorporate new observations -- we propose a dynamic state reset, triggered by the cumulative magnitude of state updates and the model's prediction confidence and accompanied by an anchor-to-world alignment. Our method achieves consistent improvements on camera pose estimation, video depth estimation, and 3D reconstruction, while substantially mitigating the performance degradation in the long sequence evaluation. Notably, on KITTI Odometry, our method achieves on average 1.68x lower ATE than LongStream, demonstrating its robustness on extended outdoor sequences.

</details>


### [71] [PRIME: Perception Feedback with Situational Memory Embeddings in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22040)
*Erik Deinzer,Naya Baslan,Luca Paparusso,Narunas Vaskevicius,Peter Knott,Luigi Palmieri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRIME，一种学习式反馈机制，利用情境记忆让VLA感知查询受下游推理与导航目标调制，在Bench2Drive上取得82.47驾驶得分与60.00%成功率，刷新公开VLA最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶VLA模型沿感知—推理—规划层级前馈推理，感知模块虽有时间递归，但早期感知对下游推理和导航目标"盲视"，只能无差别处理视觉输入，无法根据先前决策优先关注关键线索，导致感知与意图脱节。

Method: PRIME以"情境记忆"（Situational Memory）为条件，通过交叉注意力聚合过去L步窗口内的感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为的潜表征，对VLA感知查询施加学习式反馈，实现意图驱动的感知注意力；仅增加最多29.7M参数，占7.3B基础模型的0.41%。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环基准上，PRIME取得82.47的驾驶得分（较ORION提升4.73）和60.00%的成功率（提升5.38个百分点），为基于Think2Drive示范训练的已发表VLA中最高驾驶得分。

Conclusion: 将下游意图与历史决策反馈至早期感知，可在极低参数开销下显著提升VLA闭环驾驶性能，验证了意图驱动感知注意力机制的有效性。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving operate primarily through feedforward inference across the perception--reasoning--planning hierarchy. While modern architectures maintain temporal recurrence within the perceptual module, early perception remains blind to downstream reasoning and navigation goals, processing visual inputs agnostically without prioritizing cues informed by prior decisions. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces PRIME, a learned feedback mechanism that conditions the VLA perceptual queries on a novel Situational Memory. By aggregating latent representations of past perception, reasoning, navigation goals, and predicted behaviors across an L-step window via cross-attention, PRIME enables intent-driven perceptual attention at minimal computational cost, adding only a maximum of 29.7M parameters (0.41% of the 7.3B-parameter base model). Evaluated on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, PRIME achieves a state-of-the-art Driving Score of 82.47 (+4.73 over ORION) and a Success Rate of 60.00% (+5.38 percentage points), the highest reported Driving Score among published VLAs trained on Think2Drive demonstrations.

</details>


### [72] [Traffic Sign Recognition for Autonomous Driving Using Branched YOLOv2 and Geometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22060)
*Arefeh Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于YOLOv2的交通标志检测与分类系统：通过中间预测层分支实现早期退出以降低推理时间，并利用无监督贝叶斯分割与几何模板匹配减少相似标志误分类。在GTSDB/GTSRB构建的10类数据集上，分支结构达0.647 s/0.680 mAP（基线0.6607 s/0.680 mAP），推理阶段几何验证将mAP提升至0.713。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志识别需同时完成定位与分类，且自动驾驶/ADAS对实时性要求高。YOLOv2基线在简单场景仍需完整计算，且对视觉相似标志易误分类，因此需要兼顾效率与精度的改进方案。

Method: 以YOLOv2为检测框架。①在YOLOv2中加入中间预测层，形成分支网络，使简单样本可提前终止推理；研究整图分支与单元格分支两种策略。②引入几何信息：用无监督贝叶斯图像分割生成二值表示，在YOLOv2边界框内与类别特定几何模板比对，作为推理阶段验证或训练额外信号。数据方面，融合GTSDB与GTSRB，采用无缝克隆和可控图像变换构建10类数据集，含3000训练与300测试样本。

Result: 所选分支结构运行时间0.647 s、mAP 0.680；基线YOLOv2为0.6607 s、0.680 mAP。推理阶段几何验证使mAP升至0.713；几何特征训练变体mAP 0.697，运行时间0.6608 s。

Conclusion: 分支式YOLOv2可在不损失mAP的情况下降低推理时间；几何模板验证能显著减少相似标志误分类并提升mAP，其中推理阶段几何验证效果最佳。系统在效率与精度之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition (TSR) is an important perception task for autonomous driving and advanced driver-assistance systems, where a system must both localize traffic signs and determine their semantic classes efficiently. This work presents a TSR system based on YOLOv2 for simultaneous detection and classification. Two complementary modifications are studied. First, YOLOv2 is extended with intermediate prediction layers, forming a branched architecture that can terminate inference early for easy cases and reduce computation time. Both whole-image and cell-wise branching strategies are investigated. Second, geometric information is introduced to reduce classification errors between visually similar signs. An unsupervised Bayesian image-segmentation method produces binary representations that are compared with class-specific geometric templates inside YOLOv2 bounding boxes. This information is used either during inference or as an additional signal during training. A dedicated dataset is constructed by combining GTSDB and GTSRB samples using seamless cloning and controlled image transformations. Experiments cover ten traffic-sign classes, with 3,000 training and 300 test samples. The selected branched architecture reports 0.647 s runtime and 0.680 mAP, compared with 0.6607 s and 0.680 mAP for baseline YOLOv2. Geometric verification during inference increases mAP to 0.713, while the geometric-feature training variant achieves 0.697 mAP with a reported runtime of 0.6608 s.

</details>


### [73] [OmniVBench: A Benchmark and Large-Scale Dataset for Omni Reference-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22069)
*Wenxue Li,Peiyan Guan,Haoyang Jiang,Junxian Cai,Hualuo Liu,Chunjie Zhang,Chong Guan,Songlian Li,Taiyi Wu,Yongjian Yu,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Eric Liu,Xi Chen,Yu Liu,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对全模态参考到视频（omni R2V）生成，提出评测基准 OmniVBench 与训练数据集 Omni-R2V Dataset，以弥补现有基准在参考类型、组合与评估维度上的不足，并揭示当前先进模型的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考到视频（R2V）生成正朝更通用、更多样参考控制发展，形成 omni R2V 新范式；但现有基准测试覆盖的参考类型与组合有限，评估协议多停留在整体参考一致性，忽视参考因素是否被正确保留、解耦并路由到目标；同时 omni R2V 训练数据构建成本高、可用资源稀缺。

Method: 提出 OmniVBench 评测基准与 Omni-R2V Dataset 训练资源。OmniVBench 扩展参考类型、细粒度控制任务与参考组合，覆盖 7 个任务族、18 个细粒度任务，涉及内容、运动、风格、结构、叙事与多参考设置；采用 factor-grounded evaluation，包含 12,172 个样本特定检查项，评估参考因素是否被忠实保留、正确解耦并绑定到目标、且按指令实现。Omni-R2V Dataset 主要基于大规模专业视频语料，包含 340K 处理后的训练样本，覆盖多样参考类型与多参考组合，并开发任务特定管线进行参考-目标对构建，形成可扩展的数据构建方案。

Result: 对先进开源与闭源 R2V 模型进行广泛评测，在 OmniVBench 上观察到不同任务族与评估维度间存在明显性能差距，表明当前 R2V 模型仍存在局限。

Conclusion: OmniVBench 与 Omni-R2V Dataset 为 omni R2V 的评测与训练提供了更全面、细粒度的基准和工业级数据资源，并通过系统评测揭示现有模型在参考保留、解耦、绑定与任务泛化方面的不足，为后续研究提供方向。

Abstract: Reference-to-video (R2V) generation is evolving toward increasingly general and versatile reference control, giving rise to the emerging paradigm of omni R2V generation. However, existing benchmarks fall short of these emerging capabilities: their test cases cover limited reference types and compositions, and their evaluation protocols largely assess holistic reference consistency, overlooking whether reference factors are properly preserved, disentangled, and routed. Meanwhile, the high cost of constructing omni R2V training data makes suitable training resources scarce. To address these gaps, we introduce OmniVBench and the Omni-R2V Dataset for evaluating and training omni R2V models. OmniVBench expands R2V evaluation across broader reference types, fine-grained control tasks, and richer reference compositions, covering 7 task families and 18 fine-grained tasks spanning content, motion, style, structure, narrative, and multi-reference settings. We introduce factor-grounded evaluation with 12,172 case-specific checklist items, assessing whether intended reference factors are faithfully preserved, correctly disentangled and bound to their targets, and properly realized according to the instruction. We further introduce the Omni-R2V Dataset, bringing industrial-grade training resources for diverse R2V tasks to the broader research community. Drawing primarily on a large-scale corpus of professional video footage, it comprises 340K processed training samples spanning diverse reference types and multi-reference compositions. We develop task-specific pipelines for reference-target pair construction, offering a practical and scalable recipe for omni R2V data construction. Extensive evaluation of advanced open- and closed-source R2V models reveals clear performance gaps across task families and evaluation dimensions on OmniVBench, highlighting remaining limitations of current R2V models.

</details>


### [74] [MintAct: A Unified Visual Agent for Digital Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22083)
*Mingfei Gao,Rui Tian,Haiming Gang,Bohan Zhai,Le Zhang,Yuanzheng Gong,Di Feng,Ege Özsoy,Kaixin Ma,Vishwesh Kirthivasan,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Anders Boesen Lindbo Larsen,Afshin Dehghan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MintAct 是一系列 2B/4B/8B 视觉语言模型，统一了 UI 元素定位、跨移动/桌面/Web 的多步导航与视觉工具使用；借助可扩展环境与异步强化学习基础设施，在同等模型规模下匹配各领域专用模型，并在 OSWorld-Verified 上取得 48.9 的 SOTA 成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UI 智能体多按领域或能力构建专用模型，缺少统一的跨域能力，且缺乏可扩展的环境与强化学习训练基础设施；论文希望在单一视觉语言模型家族中统一 UI 定位、多步导航和视觉工具使用，同时不牺牲各领域性能。

Method: 从环境、数据和训练配方三方面进行设计。环境侧托管数百个并发实例，覆盖异构的按领域后端，同时服务于轨迹数据采集与在线强化学习；训练侧提出异步 RL 框架，显式控制跨域训练分布，并在噪声环境反馈与 off-policy 漂移下保持稳定。

Result: 在 2B、4B、8B 三种规模上，MintAct 在上述能力上均匹配各领域专用模型；在广泛基准上于同等模型规模取得 SOTA，其中 OSWorld-Verified 达到 48.9。

Conclusion: 统一视觉语言模型配合可扩展的异步强化学习基础设施，可在多 UI 域与视觉工具使用任务上达到专用专家水平，并具备良好的规模扩展性。

Abstract: We present MintAct, a family of vision-language models that unifies UI grounding, multi-step navigation across mobile, desktop, and web, and visual tool use, trained at 2B, 4B, and 8B scales. Through careful design of our environments, data, and training recipes, MintAct models match the performance of per-domain specialists across all of these capabilities. To enable this, we develop a scalable environment and reinforcement learning (RL) infrastructure. On the environment side, we host hundreds of concurrent instances across heterogeneous per-domain backends, serving both trajectory data collection and online RL. To enable efficient and scalable RL training, an asynchronous framework keeps explicit control over the cross-domain training distribution and remains stable under noisy environment feedback and off-policy drift. Experimental results show that MintAct achieves state-of-the-art performance (48.9 on OSWorld-Verified) across a wide range of benchmarks at comparable model sizes.

</details>


### [75] [Training-Adaptive Convolutional Sparse Coding via Information Bottleneck for Robust Visual Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19122)
*Meng'en Qin,Yinchen Liu,Mingxuan Cui,Youlu Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练自适应的卷积稀疏编码（CSC）框架，将稀疏系数作为可微变量与网络参数联合学习，并引入无标签后训练策略，在保持干净数据识别性能的同时显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉信号需要紧凑且充分的表征以支撑鲁棒的下游预测。卷积稀疏编码（CSC）能显式抑制冗余成分并保留信号内容，但其稀疏系数通常固定且依赖人工选取，缺乏自适应性，限制了表征质量与鲁棒性。

Method: 1）用快速迭代收缩阈值算法（FISTA）展开CSC优化过程，将稀疏系数视为可微变量，与网络参数联合学习；2）从信息瓶颈视角解释该系数：稀疏项促进紧凑表征（压缩），重建项与任务损失共同保留任务相关信号内容（信息保留），系数控制二者的权衡；3）引入无标签的后训练策略，在固定主网络参数的情况下，针对受污染输入调整压缩强度。

Result: 在CIFAR和ImageNet上的实验表明，该方法在干净数据上取得有竞争力的识别性能，并在不同输入扰动下大幅提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将稀疏系数从固定超参数转变为可学习变量，并通过信息瓶颈视角统一压缩与信息保留的权衡，配合无标签后训练自适应调整压缩强度，是一种有效的鲁棒视觉信号表征方案。

Abstract: Visual signals require compact yet sufficient representations for robust downstream prediction. Convolutional sparse coding (CSC) provides an explicit mechanism for suppressing redundant components while preserving signal content, but its sparsity coefficient is typically fixed and manually selected. We propose a training-adaptive convolutional sparse coding framework for robust visual signal representation. Specifically, we unfold the CSC optimization with the Fast Iterative Shrinkage-Thresholding Algorithm (FISTA) and treat the sparsity coefficient as a differentiable variable jointly learned with the network parameters. From the information bottleneck perspective, this coefficient controls the trade-off between information retention and compression: the sparsity term promotes compact representations, while the reconstruction term together with task loss preserves task-relevant signal content. We further introduce a label-free post-training strategy that adjusts the compression strength for corrupted inputs with the main network parameters fixed. Experiments on CIFAR and ImageNet demonstrate competitive clean-data recognition and greatly improved robustness under different input perturbations.

</details>


### [76] [AnyviewMeter: Adapting Robotic Reward Models with Camera Geometry and Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20106)
*Yuang Tu,Runjia Tan,Yujie Yan,Jinghan Hu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnyviewMeter：一种几何条件化的机器人奖励模型适配框架，通过低秩微调、token 对齐的 Plücker 射线编码与同步块注意力，将相机几何与多视角证据注入预训练奖励模型，显著提升跨视角任务进度预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型从视觉观测评估任务执行，但其预测会随相机视角和遮挡变化而改变，即使底层任务状态未变。因此将预训练奖励模型适配到特定局部任务时，必须显式建模"该任务如何被观测"这一问题。

Method: AnyviewMeter 以标量奖励表示任务进度，结合三部分：(1) 低秩微调（LoRA 式参数高效适配）；(2) token 对齐的 Plücker 射线条件化，将相机几何注入视觉特征以及注意力的 query 和 key；(3) 同步块注意力（synchronous block attention），在预训练解码器内部融合同步视角。框架同时支持单视角奖励预测与联合多视角评估，并对预训练 Robometer 模型进行参数高效适配。

Result: 在 PickCube 上，单视角适配在所有相机组中均提升进度预测，视野变化下平均绝对误差相较 RGB 微调降低约 21%。在仿真操作任务中，联合多视角预测相比对单视角 RGB 预测取平均，进度误差降低 41–69%，并在约 88% 的任务-相机组中改善时间排序。在固定相机与腕部相机的真实任务上，平均绝对误差相比平均 RGB 微调降低约 21%。

Conclusion: 相机几何与联合视觉证据是任务特定机器人奖励适配的有效组成部分，可在跨视角、跨遮挡场景下提升奖励模型的鲁棒性与多视角评估能力。

Abstract: Robotic reward models evaluate task execution from visual observations, but their predictions can change with camera viewpoint and occlusion even when the underlying task state is unchanged. Adapting a pretrained reward model to a local task therefore requires accounting for how that task is observed. We introduce AnyviewMeter, a geometry-conditioned adaptation framework for robotic reward models that represent task progress as a scalar reward signal. It combines low-rank fine-tuning with token-aligned Plucker rays and synchronous block attention: ray conditioning incorporates camera geometry into visual features and attention queries and keys, while block attention fuses synchronized views inside the pretrained decoder. The framework supports both single-view reward prediction and joint multi-view evaluation through parameter-efficient adaptation of a pretrained Robometer model. On PickCube, single-view adaptation improves progress prediction in every camera group and reduces mean absolute error under a changed field of view by approximately 21% relative to RGB fine-tuning. Across simulated manipulation tasks, joint multi-view prediction reduces progress error by 41-69% compared with averaging single-view RGB predictions and improves temporal ordering in approximately 88% of task-camera groups. On real tasks with fixed and wrist-mounted cameras, mean absolute error decreases by approximately 21% relative to averaged RGB fine-tuning. These results support camera geometry and joint visual evidence as useful components of task-specific robotic reward adaptation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [WM-VS: Progress-Aligned World Models for Closed-Loop Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20892)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yixuan Zhou,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 WM-VS：面向闭环视觉伺服的目标中心、进度对齐世界模型框架，通过离线目标区域 DINOv2 对应构造带符号四维伺服坐标（平移、尺度、面内旋转），先让动作条件潜变量转移与该坐标对齐，再冻结世界模型训练反应式关节速度策略，结合动作模仿、后果监督与短程想象滚动以促进误差收缩；在真实 7 自由度 eye-to-hand 系统上实现高成功率闭环修正与对未见 3D 目标的零重训练迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环视觉伺服中，世界模型预测常只关注动作是否“合理”，而不判断该动作是否真正降低任务误差，形成“预测-控制错配”（prediction-control mismatch）；需要一种能对齐动作后果与任务进度、直接服务于误差收缩的预测与控制框架。

Method: WM-VS 以目标为中心构造进度对齐世界模型：1）利用离线目标区域 DINOv2 对应关系定义带符号的四维伺服坐标，覆盖平移、尺度与面内旋转；2）Stage 1 将动作条件潜变量转移与该伺服坐标对齐；3）Stage 2 冻结世界模型，训练反应式关节速度策略，采用动作模仿、后果监督和短程想象滚动，使滚动结果偏向误差收缩；部署仅需 RGB、反应式执行，无需在线轨迹优化。

Result: 在真实 7-DoF eye-to-hand 系统上，WM-VS 在 30/30 次试验中将角点 RMSE 降至不超过初始值的 10%，并在最终有效帧仍保持该标准 25/30（83.33%）；移除未来误差对齐后，保持率降至 26.67%。学习到的进度信号与未用于训练或控制的外部 AprilTag 角点误差一致（平均 Spearman rho=0.8778）。无需重训练，两个未见 3D 目标分别取得平移误差降低 86.48% 与 90.27%，旋转误差降低 70.01% 与 65.70%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将动作后果与进度对齐能够支撑重复闭环修正，并提升对未见目标的迁移能力；代码与数据将开源发布。

Abstract: Closed-loop visual servoing requires predictions that indicate whether an action reduces task error, not only whether the action is plausible. We call this gap the prediction-control mismatch and introduce WM-VS, a target-centric progress-aligned world-model framework for closed-loop visual servoing. Offline target-region DINOv2 correspondences define a signed four-dimensional servo coordinate for translation, scale, and in-plane rotation. Stage 1 aligns action-conditioned latent transitions with this coordinate; Stage 2 freezes the world model and trains a reactive joint-velocity policy with action imitation, consequence supervision, and short imagined rollouts that favor error contraction. Deployment is RGB-only and reactive, without online trajectory optimization. On a real 7-DoF eye-to-hand system, WM-VS reaches a corner RMSE no larger than 10 percent of its initial value in 30/30 trials and retains this criterion at the final valid frame in 25/30 (83.33 percent). Removing future-error alignment reduces retention to 26.67 percent. The learned progress signal agrees with an external AprilTag corner error not used for training or control (mean Spearman rho = 0.8778). Without retraining, two unseen 3D targets achieve translation-error reductions of 86.48 percent and 90.27 percent and rotation-error reductions of 70.01 percent and 65.70 percent. These results link progress-aligned action consequences to repeated closed-loop correction and transfer. Code and data will be released as open source.

</details>


### [78] [AeRove: A Compact Bimodal Aerial-Terrestrial Drone with Rapid Bistable Reconfiguration for Close-Range Pipeline Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20965)
*Caleb Polillio,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeRove：一种紧凑型空地双模机器人，用桨叶保护罩作轮在管道上滚动，弹簧双稳态机构200 ms内完成模式切换且无需持续驱动功率；将飞行仅用于越障、地面滚动用于连续行进，使电流降低14倍、估计行程由144 m增至2057 m，并在51 cm钢管上验证导航、越障与泄漏检测；CO2传感实验表明栖息检测响应强于悬停，飞行中底部桨叶进气构型响应更快更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准无人机难以进行近距离管道检测，主要受限于悬停功耗高、对气流敏感，以及螺旋桨尾流干扰气体传感。因此需要一种兼顾连续贴管移动与越障能力、并改善气体检测性能的平台。

Method: 提出AeRove紧凑型空地双模机器人：以螺旋桨保护罩作为轮子在管道上滚动；采用弹簧加载双稳态机构，在200 ms内完成地面与飞行模式重构，且维持任一状态无需持续执行器功率；收敛式桨叶保护罩几何提升实测推力效率。运动策略上，将飞行保留用于越障跳跃，用地面滚动实现连续通行。

Result: 相比连续飞行，电流消耗降低14倍（0.7 A vs. 10 A），估计行程由144 m延长至2057 m。在直径51 cm钢管上的自主试验验证了直线与弯曲段导航、越障跳跃以及对模拟检测标记的泄漏检测。独立CO2传感实验评估栖息与空中工况下的气体检测性能：测试条件下栖息检测的浓度响应显著大于悬停；飞行时底部螺旋桨进气构型借助桨叶诱导气流，比外伸探头产生更快、更强的气体检测响应。

Conclusion: AeRove通过空地双模滚动/飞行与低功耗双稳态重构，有效降低管道近距离检测的能耗并扩展行程，同时利用栖息与桨叶诱导气流提升气体检测响应。代码与设计以CC-BY许可发布。

Abstract: Close-proximity pipeline inspection is challenging for standard drones due to high hovering power consumption, airflow sensitivity, and propeller wash interference with gas sensing. To address this, we present AeRove, a compact bimodal aerial-terrestrial robot. AeRove uses its propeller guards as wheels to roll along pipes and employs a spring-loaded bistable mechanism to reconfigure between ground and flight modes in 200 ms without continuous actuator power to maintain either state. The converging propeller-guard geometry also improves measured thrust efficiency. By reserving flight for obstacle hopping and using ground rolling for continuous traversal, current draw is reduced 14x compared to continuous flight (0.7 A vs. 10 A), extending estimated travel distance from 144 m to 2057 m. Autonomous trials on a 51 cm diameter steel pipe demonstrated navigation on straight and curved sections, obstacle jumping, and leak detection of simulated inspection markers. Separate CO2 sensing experiments evaluated gas-detection performance under perched and aerial operating conditions. Perched inspection produced a substantially larger concentration response than hovering under the tested conditions. During flight, an underbody propeller-intake configuration produced a faster and stronger gas-detection response than an extended probe by leveraging propeller-induced airflow. Code and designs are released under the CC-BY license.

</details>


### [79] [Shake to Learn: Dynamic Interrogation of Hidden Object Physics for Robotic Manipulation with Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20970)
*Wen Sin Lor,Jun Wang,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出"摇动学习"（shake-to-learn）机械 interrogate 框架：用折纸启发的软体机械臂作为物理储备池计算机，对抓取物体施加固定摇动激励并记录振铃响应，通过轻量线性读出解码隐藏物体物理属性（如质心方向与距离），并用于后续重抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作所需的许多物理属性无法由视觉直接获得，例如密封物体的质心位置或内部内容物，通常需通过提举、摇动或动态扰动才能显现。因此需要一种新的感知与学习模态，将交互诱发的动态响应转化为对传统传感不可见物体物理量的推断依据。

Method: 实现方式为折纸启发的软体机械臂，将其作为物理储备池计算机。抓取物体后，在机械臂基座施加固定摇动输入，并通过相机跟踪或嵌入式传感器记录 resulting ringdown 响应。由于输入在各试验中保持一致，隐藏物体属性（如质心位置）通过其对机器人—物体耦合系统动力学的影响被编码；随后用轻量线性读出解码这些动力学，恢复可解释的隐藏物理信息。作者还提出振铃响应的动态摘要表示以提升预测精度。任务包括：推断隐藏质心方向、推断质心到抓取点的距离、以及利用推断的质心信息指导后续重抓取。

Result: 软体机械臂储备池完成了三个难度递增的任务：推断物体隐藏质心方向、推断质心距离抓取点的距离、以及利用推断质心信息进行后续重抓取。所提出的动态摘要表示进一步提高了预测准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将短暂物理交互转化为关于隐藏物体属性的可操作线索，是机器人系统下游操作的一种有前景策略；"摇动学习"机械 interrogate 为机器人感知与学习提供了新模态。

Abstract: Many physical properties relevant to robotic manipulation are hidden from vision. A sealed object, for example, may reveal little about its center of mass (COM) or internal contents until it is lifted, shaken, or otherwise dynamically perturbed. This study shows that such interactions can enable a new modality of robotic perception and learning, in which interaction-induced dynamic responses are used to infer object physics that is inaccessible to conventional sensing. We implement this idea using an origami-inspired soft robotic arm that functions as a physical reservoir computer. After grasping an object, the arm is excited by a fixed shaking input at its base, and the resulting ringdown response is recorded through either camera tracking or embedded sensors. Because the input is held constant across trials, hidden object properties, such as the COM position, are encoded through their effect on the dynamics of the coupled robot-object system. A lightweight linear readout can then decode these dynamics to recover interpretable information about the hidden object physics. Using this framework, the soft robotic arm reservoir completed three tasks of increasing difficulty: inferring the orientation of the object's hidden COM, inferring the COM distance from the grasp point, and using the inferred COM information to guide a subsequent regrasp. We further develop a dynamic summary representation of the ringdown response that improves prediction accuracy. Together, these results establish shake-to-learn mechanical interrogation as a promising strategy for robotic systems to convert brief physical interactions into actionable cues about hidden object properties for downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [80] [ForeTac-VLA: A Forecasting-Based Tactile-Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20980)
*Zhengyu Tao,Xin Li,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ForeTac-VLA：一种基于触觉预测的触觉-视觉-语言融合模型，通过预测未来触觉状态来指导动作生成，在四类真实接触密集操作任务中平均成功率达 95%，显著优于微调 VLA 与现有触觉增强 VLA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中表现较强，但依赖视觉感知，在接触密集环境中鲁棒性受限，因为关键物理交互状态可能无法从视觉中观测。现有触觉增强 VLA 方法虽利用观测到的触觉反馈提升物理 grounding，但大多是被动反应式，未显式建模接触将如何演化。

Method: ForeTac-VLA 将近期触觉观测编码为时序表示，并通过双向交叉注意力与视觉-语言特征融合；使用基于 Transformer 的预测模块预测多步未来触觉状态，使模型能够联合推理已观测接触与预期接触；将融合后的多模态表示与预测的未来触觉状态输入 VLA 主干以条件化动作生成；训练中采用从真值到预测的课程策略，以在早期预测不可靠时稳定训练。

Result: 在四类真实世界接触密集操作任务中，ForeTac-VLA 平均成功率为 95%，比微调 VLA 模型高 36.25 个百分点，比最先进的触觉增强 VLA 基线高 22 个百分点以上；在低照度和视觉杂乱条件下仍保持较强性能。视频演示见 https://foretac-vla.github.io/。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测未来触觉状态并将其与视觉-语言特征融合，ForeTac-VLA 将触觉增强 VLA 从反应式提升为可预测式，有效增强接触密集操作任务的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet their reliance on visual perception limits robustness in contact-rich environments, where critical physical interaction states may not be visually observable. Existing tactile-enhanced VLA methods improve physical grounding using observed tactile feedback, but most remain largely reactive rather than explicitly modeling how contact may evolve. Therefore, we propose ForeTac-VLA, a forecasting-based tactile-vision-language fusion model that predicts future tactile states to guide action generation. Specifically, ForeTac-VLA encodes recent tactile observations into temporal representations and integrates them with vision-language features through bidirectional cross-attention. Further, a transformer-based forecasting module predicts multi-step future tactile states, enabling the model to reason jointly over observed and anticipated contact. Finally, the fused multimodal representations and predicted future tactile states are fed into the VLA backbone to condition action generation. To stabilize training, a ground-truth-to-prediction curriculum is employed when early forecasts are unreliable. Across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ForeTac-VLA achieves an average success rate of 95%, outperforming the fine-tuned VLA model by 36.25 percentage points and state-of-the-art tactile-enhanced VLA baselines by over 22 percentage points. ForeTac-VLA also maintains strong performance under low-illumination and visually cluttered conditions. Video demonstrations can be found on https://foretac-vla.github.io/

</details>


### [81] [PIVOT: Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability for Field Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20983)
*Aoran Jiao,Wenda Zhao,Hshmat Sahak,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIVOT 将视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义地形评估与几何规划结合，并用真实测量校准各模态权重；在约 6.4 km 混合地形闭环试验中，将自主率从 59.6% 提升到 97.0%，人工干预从 11 次降至 3 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野移动机器人需要在非结构化、几何复杂环境中安全可靠导航。传统基于几何的地形评估计算快，但在非结构化场景中往往过于保守，限制了自主导航能力，因此需要引入语义理解来补充几何信息。

Method: 提出 PIVOT：一种物理信息引导的视觉语言越野可通行性导航系统。该方法用 VLM 进行语义推理，预测通行能量代价、机器人振动和车轮滑移，并量化这些预测与真实测量的相关性；据此构建统一可通行性评分，按各模态与实测的相关性加权。为提升效率，设计两级导航架构：以几何规划为常规模式，仅当几何模式无法找到路径时才触发语义重规划。

Result: 在约 6.4 km 的混合地形路线上进行 5 次重复闭环试验。与仅几何导航相比，系统整体自主率从 59.6% 提升至 97.0%，人工干预次数从 11 次降至 3 次，平均干预间隔距离（MDBI）从 69.2 m 提升至 412.9 m。

Conclusion: 结果表明，物理信息引导的 VLM 地形评估能够显著突破纯几何方法的自主导航局限，同时通过保留几何规划作为常规模式维持高效规划能力。

Abstract: Terrain assessment is a critical capability for off-road mobile robots, enabling safe and reliable navigation through unstructured and geometrically complex environments. Conventional geometry-based terrain assessment is fast to compute but often overly conservative in unstructured environments. We present PIVOT: a Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability navigation system that augments conventional geometry-based planning with vision-language-model (VLM)-based semantic reasoning for field robots. To physically ground this assessment, we quantify how strongly the VLM's predicted traversal energy cost, robot vibration, and wheel slip correlate with real-world measurements and introduce a unified traversability score that weights each modality by its prediction-measurement correlation. For efficiency, we design a two-level navigation architecture that retains geometry-based planning as the nominal mode and invokes semantic replanning only when that mode fails to find a path. Across five repeated closed-loop trials on a mixed-terrain route totalling around $6.4$ km, the proposed system increases overall autonomy from $59.6\%$ to $97.0\%$, reduces human interventions from $11$ to $3$, and increases the mean distance between interventions (MDBI) from $69.2$ m to $412.9$ m compared with geometry-only navigation. These results demonstrate that physically grounded VLM-based terrain assessment can substantially extend autonomous navigation beyond the limitations of geometry alone, while preserving efficient geometric planning as the nominal mode.

</details>


### [82] [Do Spinning Radar Doppler Velocity Measurements Improve Vehicle Detection and Tracking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21000)
*Eric Xie,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究旋转式FMCW雷达的多普勒速度测量能否提升车辆检测与跟踪性能；通过自动标注流水线生成真值数据，证明多普勒去畸变可提升检测精度，多普勒速度先验可显著提升跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转式FMCW雷达因对恶劣天气鲁棒且具备360°视场，在自动驾驶感知中日益流行，近期还被证明可生成逐方位角的多普勒速度。然而，这些多普勒速度测量是否能真正提升旋转雷达的车辆检测与跟踪性能尚不清楚，且目前缺乏带动态目标真值标签的多普勒旋转雷达数据集。

Method: 检测方面：估计自车运动（ego motion），据此在输入网络前消除雷达图像的多普勒距离畸变（Doppler undistortion）。跟踪方面：提出一种新的逐车辆速度估计方法，并将其作为跟踪器运动模型的先验。数据方面：由于没有现成的带真值动态目标标签的多普勒旋转雷达数据集，作者构建了自动标注流水线，用一组微调的现成激光雷达检测器对Boreas Road Trip数据集全部643 km进行标注，再将检测结果迁移到雷达，取超过250 km的车辆密集序列作为真值训练数据。

Result: 训练并评估两种最先进检测器后，多普勒去畸变可使检测精度提升最高2.37个mAP点；多普勒速度先验相较零速度初始化基线可将跟踪精度提升13.68个MOTA点，同时达到使用真值速度作为先验所获MOTA的99.7%。

Conclusion: 多普勒速度测量能有效改善旋转式FMCW雷达的车辆检测与跟踪性能：去畸变提升检测精度，逐车辆速度先验显著提升跟踪精度，且接近使用真值速度的理想上限。同时，所提出的自动标注流水线为多普勒旋转雷达感知研究提供了可用的真值数据来源。

Abstract: Spinning frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have been gaining popularity in autonomous vehicle perception on account of their robustness to adverse weather conditions and 360° field of view. Recently, scanning radars have also been shown capable of generating per-azimuth Doppler velocity. In this paper, we investigate whether these Doppler velocity measurements improve spinning radar vehicle detection and tracking performance. For detection, we estimate the ego motion and use it to undo the Doppler range distortion of the radar image before passing it to a network. For tracking, we propose a new way to estimate a per-vehicle velocity and use it as a prior for the tracker's motion model. Since Doppler-enabled spinning radar data is not available in any dataset with ground-truth dynamic object labels, our first contribution is an automatic labelling pipeline that uses an ensemble of fine-tuned off-the-shelf lidar detectors to label all 643 km of the Boreas Road Trip dataset. We then transfer detections to radar, and use over 250 km of vehicle-dense sequences as ground-truth training data. By training and evaluating two state-of-the-art detectors, we show that Doppler undistortion can improve detection accuracy by up to $2.37$ points on mean average precision. Furthermore, we show that the Doppler velocity prior can improve tracking accuracy by $13.68$ points on multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA) versus the zero-velocity initialization baseline, while achieving $99.7\%$ of the MOTA obtained using ground-truth velocities as the prior.

</details>


### [83] [Project SCOUT: Interceptor Drone for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21005)
*Azmain Yousuf,Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Lifeng Zhou,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT：一种ROS集成的机载感知与控制框架，用于对来袭无人机的实时空中防御，在防御无人机上直接完成检测、关联、跟踪滤波与控制指令生成，无需外部感知设施或地面站计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经授权的无人机快速增多，周界防御对鲁棒、抗干扰的反无人机系统需求日益增长；同时依赖外部感知基础设施或地面站计算会限制系统部署灵活性与实时性，因此需要一套完全机载、实时的视觉跟踪与闭环控制方案。

Method: 提出SCOUT（Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking）框架：在防御无人机机载端完成视觉检测、目标关联、轨迹滤波与控制指令生成。感知流水线将TensorRT加速的无人机检测与基于ByteTrack的关联以及轻量级轨迹保留状态机相结合；状态机用于拒绝突变的目标跳变，并在检测暂时退化时维持短期目标连续性，从而减少误检测或目标切换引起的不稳定控制响应。系统通过ROS集成，并采用平面"守门"（goalkeeping）拦截策略进行闭环控制。

Result: 在真实硬件部署的平面"守门"拦截场景中，防御无人机跟踪来袭目标并调整运动以在受保护边界附近维持拦截构型。实飞结果表明，SCOUT在实时机载检测速率下，有效目标检测帧占比达92.2%，验证了面向无人机周界防御的视觉跟踪与闭环控制的可行性。

Conclusion: SCOUT证明了仅依靠机载感知与计算即可实现实时的反无人机视觉跟踪与闭环控制，为周界防御提供了可行方案；作者提供了端到端周界防御运行的视频演示。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of unauthorized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created a growing need for robust, jamming-resistant counter-UAV systems for perimeter defense. This paper presents \textbf{SCOUT} (Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking), a ROS-integrated onboard perception and control framework for real-time aerial defense against incoming UAVs. SCOUT performs visual detection, target association, track filtering, and control command generation directly onboard the defender UAV, without relying on external sensing infrastructure or ground-station computation. To provide stable control inputs, the perception pipeline combines TensorRT-accelerated drone detection with ByteTrack-based association and a lightweight track-retention state machine. The state machine rejects abrupt target jumps and maintains short-term target continuity during temporary detection degradation, reducing unstable control responses caused by false detections or target switching. We evaluate the proposed architecture through an integrated hardware deployment executing a planar ``goalkeeping'' interception strategy. In this setting, the defender UAV tracks the incoming target and adjusts its motion to maintain a blocking configuration near the protected boundary. Real-world flight results show that SCOUT maintains valid target detections for 92.2\% of frames while operating at real-time onboard detection rates, demonstrating the feasibility of visual tracking and closed-loop control for UAV perimeter defense. A video demonstration of the end-to-end perimeter defense operation is available online. https://figshare.com/s/497befc033091fc0b84f

</details>


### [84] [SPARROW: Survival-POMCP for Adaptive Robot Routing, Observation, and Waiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21008)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将临时障碍物下的机器人导航建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），提出基于POMCP的信念空间规划器SPARROW，联合决策绕行、观测与有限时长等待，并在线学习右删失生存模型，在仿真与实体机器人上显著降低到达目标时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在执行既定路线时可能遭遇临时阻塞（如可移动障碍、临时封闭），此时需要在线判断应等待障碍清除、改道绕行，还是主动获取更多障碍信息。现有方法（如基于生存分析的OSCAR）难以同时处理部分可观测性、序贯决策以及障碍在备选路径上动态出现的耦合问题，因此需要一个能统一权衡等待、绕行与信息采集的规划框架。

Method: 1) 问题建模：将临时障碍物下的图导航形式化为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POSMDP），动作空间包含穿越、观测和有限时长等待三类。2) 规划器：提出SPARROW，基于部分可观测蒙特卡洛规划（POMCP）在信念空间搜索，用粒子滤波维护对潜在障碍类别与清除时间的信念。3) 学习机制：在线学习类别条件生存模型，训练数据同时来自清除观测与右删失样本（即机器人在观察到清除前已改道）。4) 生成模型：模拟各动作展开过程中障碍的到达与清除，使规划器能考虑备选路径上新出现的阻塞。5) 引入"学习价值"（value-of-learning）准则，在采集带标签生存数据的即时成本与其对未来导航后悔值的预期削减之间进行权衡。

Result: 在两个仿真图及多种障碍类别设置下，SPARROW相较同问题的近期生存分析方法OSCAR将平均到达目标时间降低12%–26%。在实体移动机器人上，SPARROW相较OSCAR降低平均到达目标时间20.5%，并能随环境条件变化自适应地在观测、等待与绕行之间切换。

Conclusion: 将临时障碍导航统一表述为POSMDP并结合POMCP信念空间规划，可有效整合等待、绕行与主动信息获取三类决策；在线右删失生存学习与学习价值准则进一步提升决策质量。仿真与真机实验表明该方法优于现有生存分析基线，具备实际部署的鲁棒性与自适应性。

Abstract: Temporary obstacles that may block a robot's planned route create a sequential navigation problem: a robot must decide whether to wait for a blockage to clear, reroute, or acquire more information about the obstacle before acting. We formulate graph navigation among temporary obstacles as a partially observable semi-Markov decision process and introduce SPARROW, a belief-space planner built on Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning (POMCP). SPARROW searches over traversal, observation, and finite-duration waiting actions while maintaining a particle belief over latent obstacle classes and clearance times. Class-conditioned survival models are learned online from both clearance observations and right-censored encounters where the robot reroutes before clearance is observed. A generative model simulates obstacle arrivals and clearances as each action unfolds, so the planner can account for blockages that may occur along alternative routes. We further introduce a value-of-learning criterion that trades the immediate cost of collecting labelled survival data against its expected reduction in future navigation regret. Across two simulation graphs and multiple obstacle-class settings, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 12-26% relative to OSCAR, a recent survival-based method for the same problem. On a physical mobile robot, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 20.5% relative to OSCAR while selectively observing, waiting, and rerouting as environment conditions change.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Effective Visual-Inertial SLAM with Passive-Only Sensors for Low-Cost Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21015)
*Grant Schwidder,David Widhalm,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明：仅用低于1万美元的消费级传感器平台，也能在完全无约束的6自由度水下环境中实现可用的VI-SLAM导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端AUV依赖DVL、USBL等昂贵传感器套件，成本门槛高，阻碍了先进海洋机器人从专业实验室向更广泛研究与爱好者群体普及。因此需要验证低成本平台是否也能实现鲁棒、高质量的水下导航。

Method: 采用成本相近的开源AUV平台，仅配备消费级传感器（立体相机、MEMS惯性测量单元、深度传感器），在完全无约束的6-DOF水下环境中进行评测；分析现成SLAM软件包的有效性，并针对无缆水下作业的视觉与物理挑战提出传感器融合优化方案。

Result: 实验表明，在无工业级硬件的条件下，低成本消费级传感器平台仍可获得可用的SLAM解，验证了其在高要求实时海上任务中的可行性。

Conclusion: VI-SLAM可被大众以低成本获取，为在严苛实时海洋任务中的性能预期提供了基准，并消除了工业级硬件的资金壁垒。

Abstract: Improvements to Visual-Inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for low-cost autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are critical for transitioning advanced marine robotics from specialized labs to broader research and hobbyist applications. While high-end AUVs typically rely on expensive sensor suites - such as Doppler Velocity Logs (DVLs) and Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) systems - this work demonstrates that robust, high-quality navigation is achievable using a sub-$10, 000(USD) platform equipped only with inexpensive consumer-grade sensors. By leveraging a similarly priced, open-source AUV, we evaluate the performance of stereo cameras, Micro-electromechanical System (MEMS)-based IMUs, and depth sensors in a fully unconstrained 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) underwater environment. We analyze the efficacy of off-the-shelf SLAM packages and propose optimizations for sensor fusion to mitigate the visual and physical challenges of untethered underwater operation. Our results prove that a usable SLAM solution can be accessible to the masses, providing a benchmark for expectations in demanding, real-time maritime missions without the financial barrier of industrial-grade hardware.

</details>


### [86] [Catch Me If You Can: Real-Time Feedback Denoising for Responsive VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21022)
*Yiheng Ji,Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLA-Feedback：一种双时间尺度架构，将低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈结合，把扩散去噪的最后一步保留为轻量反馈接口，从而在不重跑整个视觉-语言扩散模型的前提下实现实时动作纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通过结合预训练视觉-语言模型的语义知识与动作生成策略，在机器人操作中展现出较强泛化能力。但基于扩散的动作生成器虽擅长建模时序连贯的动作块，这些动作块在推理后通常以开环方式执行，当物体移动、接触状态变化或场景在执行过程中演变时，响应能力受限。

Method: VLA-Feedback 采用双时间尺度架构：低频侧使用扩散规划器生成动作块，高频侧引入视觉反馈。关键设计在于不将动作块完全去噪后再执行，而是保留其最后一步去噪作为轻量级反馈接口，使每个动作在执行前能够依据最新观测进行纠正。该设计既保留了扩散规划器的表达能力，又实现了实时动作纠正，且无需重新运行完整的视觉-语言扩散模型。

Result: 在静态 LIBERO 任务上，VLA-Feedback 与 GR00T 表现相当；在动态仿真任务上，平均成功率从 27.5% 提升至 85.0%；在真实机器人任务上，平均成功率从 51% 提升至 73%。

Conclusion: 通过将扩散规划的最后去噪步骤复用为反馈接口，VLA-Feedback 在保持扩散规划表达力的同时赋予动作执行闭环响应能力，显著提升了动态仿真与真实机器人场景下的任务成功率，验证了双时间尺度设计的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by combining semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models with expressive action-generation policies. Diffusion-based action generators are particularly effective for modeling temporally coherent action chunks, but these chunks are typically executed open-loop after inference. This limits responsiveness when objects move, contacts change, or the scene evolves during execution. We propose VLA-Feedback, a two-timescale architecture that combines low-frequency diffusion planning with high-frequency visual feedback. Rather than fully denoising an action chunk before execution, VLA-Feedback retains its final denoising step as a lightweight feedback interface, allowing each action to be corrected using the latest observation before it is executed. This design preserves the expressiveness of the diffusion planner while enabling real-time action correction without rerunning the full vision-language diffusion model. VLA-Feedback matched GR00T on static LIBERO tasks while improving average success on dynamic simulation tasks from 27.5% to 85.0%. On real-robot tasks, it improved average success from 51% to 73%. Additional materials can be found on our project page: https://vla-feedback.github.io.

</details>


### [87] [Adapting Rigid-Body Dynamics Derivatives for Constraint Embedding Closed-Chain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21024)
*Daniel J. Volpi,Patrick M. Wensing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将刚体动力学一阶导数的已有算法推广到使用约束嵌入（constraint embedding）建模的闭链运动系统，去除了"关节速度效应局部构型不变"这一假设，给出了更通用的动力学导数算法，并分析了额外项的计算代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多标准动力学算法可同时适用于开链与约束嵌入模型，但现有的高效导数方法都假设"关节速度效应在局部构型下不变"。这一假设限制了方法对更一般关节类型（尤其是约束嵌入闭链模型中的关节）的适用性。为提升动力学导数方法在闭链系统上的通用性与精度，作者希望去除该假设。

Method: 作者在已有的一阶导数算法基础上，移除局部构型不变假设，重新推导适配的导数算法，将动力学导数扩展到更一般的关节类型，包括约束嵌入闭链模型中出现的关节。随后对比传统的销关节（pin-joint）机器人模型与更完整的、能刻画局部闭链的驱动模型，评估新增项的计算开销。

Result: 由这些推广引入的额外项在仅对驱动运动学（actuation kinematics）建模时计算影响很小；但当驱动链中额外包含刚体（如电机转子）时，或考虑非局部闭环时，计算代价会显著升高。

Conclusion: 该工作使约束嵌入驱动子机构的动力学计算更加精确，可应用于模型预测控制（MPC）与可微分仿真。

Abstract: This paper extends an existing algorithm for the first-order derivatives of rigid-body dynamics to the case of closed-chain kinematic systems modeled using constraint embed- ding. Many standard dynamics algorithms apply to both open- chain and constraint-embedded models, but existing efficient derivative methods assume joint velocity effects are locally config- uration invariant. We remove this assumption and derive adapted algorithms that extend dynamics derivatives to more general joint types, including those arising in constraint-embedded closed- chain models. Our results compare conventional pin-joint robot models with more complete actuation models that capture local closed chains. We show that the additional terms introduced by these generalizations have low computational impact when mod- eling actuation kinematics alone, but can incur higher cost when additional rigid bodies, such as motor rotors, are included in the actuation chain, or when considering non-local loops. Overall, these results enable more accurate dynamics computations for constraint-embedded actuation submechanisms to be adopted in model-predictive control and differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [88] [MAPLE-RF: Efficient Probabilistic RF Source Localization in Partially Explored Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21026)
*Haozhe Lei,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在部分探索地图上的单快照射频发射源定位，比较了扩展数字孪生方法与 MAPLE-RF 两种输出发射源位置后验的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频发射源定位通常需要环境模型来预测障碍物对信号的遮挡与反射，但在机器人应用中往往只有部分地图（如 SLAM 探索过程中）。现有方法多依赖完整环境先验，难以适应部分探索地图的场景，因此需要研究在部分地图上的单快照定位方法。

Method: 论文比较两种输出发射源位置后验的方法。第一种扩展数字孪生方法：对每个候选位置进行射线追踪，将未探索空间视为自由空间，并在混合地图覆盖度上训练。第二种为 MAPLE-RF：将估计的到达路径角与信噪比编码为网格通道，与地图已知度、占据情况、视距可见性对齐，用 U-Net 一次前向打分所有候选位置，推理时不模拟传播。

Result: 室内环境射线追踪仿真表明：两种方法都必须在混合地图覆盖度上训练。数字孪生方法在多数单快照指标上更准确；MAPLE-RF 精度接近，但其查询代价不依赖传播模型，比使用通用射线追踪的全网格重新查询低两个数量级以上。两者均优于高斯与高斯混合基线；在由自身估计引导的探索路径上，融合后的 MAPLE-RF 后验在真实源附近赋予的概率更高。

Conclusion: 在部分探索地图上，数字孪生方法精度更高，而 MAPLE-RF 以极低的推理查询代价达到接近精度且不依赖传播模型，二者均优于概率基线方法，MAPLE-RF 在探索引导下表现更佳。代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Localizing a radio-frequency (RF) transmitter from received signals often requires a model of the environment to predict how obstacles block and reflect the signal. In many robotic applications, however, only a partial map is available, particularly when a robot localizes the source while exploring with simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). We study single-snapshot transmitter localization on such partially explored maps and compare two approaches that output a posterior over transmitter locations. The first extends a digital-twin method, which ray-traces every candidate location, to partial maps by treating unexplored space as free and training on mixed map coverage. The second, MAPLE-RF, encodes estimated path angles of arrival and signal-to-noise ratios as grid channels aligned with map knownness, occupancy, and line-of-sight visibility, and a U-Net scores all candidate positions in one pass without simulating propagation at inference. Ray-tracing simulations of indoor environments indicate that training on mixed map coverage is essential for both approaches. The digital-twin approach is more accurate on most single-snapshot metrics, while MAPLE-RF comes close at a query cost that does not depend on the propagation model and is more than two orders of magnitude below a fresh full-grid query with general-purpose ray tracing. Both outperform Gaussian and Gaussian-mixture baselines, and on exploration routes guided by its own estimates, fused MAPLE-RF posteriors place more probability near the source than the compared methods. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [89] [DEXTERA: From a Single Image to Deployable Dexterous Manipulation via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21045)
*Jin Wu,Lianjie Yuan,Zeyan Sun,Yuanyuan Lei,Disi A,Bicheng Han,Fangzhou Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXTERA 是一个自动化的 real-to-sim-to-real 框架，可从单张 RGB 图像构建可部署的灵巧操作策略，并通过仿真-现实协同训练显著提升真实机器人策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界灵巧操作数据采集成本高、耗时长；高保真物理仿真虽可扩展数据合成与策略学习，但手工构建可部署数字孪生劳动密集，且视觉、几何与动力学残差阻碍可靠的 sim-to-real 迁移。

Method: 提出 DEXTERA，包含四个统一阶段：(1) 单图场景分解为静态高斯背景与可交互刚体/关节体资产，并用 VLM 推断物理参数；(2) 度量场景全局对齐、物体规范化与形态平衡的机器人标定；(3) 可扩展仿真任务原语构建、VR 遥操作与以物体为中心轨迹合成；(4) 共享多模态策略接口，支持模仿学习与强化学习。

Result: 在 13 个任务-本体配对、2 个灵巧机器人平台、6 种策略架构上评估。相比生成式基线，DEXTERA 具有更优视觉保真度与 3D 几何重建；跨域轨迹回放验证了强物理交互一致性；仅仿真训练策略可实现可行的零样本真机部署；仿真-现实协同训练将平均物理策略成功率从 29.2% 提升至 61.9%。

Conclusion: DEXTERA 实现了从单张 RGB 图像到可部署灵巧操作策略的自动化 real-to-sim-to-real 流程，在视觉、几何与物理一致性上表现优异，并验证了零样本部署与协同训练对真实策略成功率的显著提升。

Abstract: Collecting real-world robot data for dexterous manipulation is costly and time-consuming. While high-fidelity physics simulators enable scalable data synthesis and policy learning, constructing deployment-ready digital twins manually remains labor-intensive, and residual visual, geometric, and dynamics gaps hinder reliable sim-to-real transfer. We present DEXTERA, an automated real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms a single RGB image into deployable policies for dexterous manipulation across four unified stages: (1) single-image scene factorization into a static Gaussian background and interactive rigid or articulated assets with VLM-inferred physical parameters; (2) metric scene global alignment, object canonicalization, and morphology-balanced robot calibration; (3) scalable simulator task primitive construction, VR teleoperation, and object-centric trajectory synthesis; and (4) a shared multimodal policy interface supporting both imitation learning and reinforcement learning. We evaluate DEXTERA across 13 task-embodiment pairs, 2 dexterous robot platforms, and 6 policy architectures. Experimental results demonstrate that DEXTERA achieves superior visual fidelity and 3D geometric reconstruction compared to generative baselines, while cross-domain trajectory replays validate strong physical interaction consistency. Furthermore, simulation-only trained policies enable viable zero-shot real-robot deployment, while simulation-real co-training substantially improves mean physical policy success from 29.2% to 61.9% across diverse policy architectures.

</details>


### [90] [Constraint-Unified MPC for Over-Actuated Surface Vehicles with Post-Detection Fault Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21046)
*Sebastian Burmester,Ruiheng Jiang,Noa Sendlhofer,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将轨迹跟踪、推力分配与单推进器力/速率约束统一到一个二次规划中的控制架构，并通过外部故障检测器提供的二值标志实现推进器故障重构；该系统已在苏黎世湖与威尼斯双年展现场演出中部署，并给出精度与容错性能的实测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编排式水上表演要求小型自主水面艇在严格的单推进器推力与速率约束下跟踪精确路径，并在推进器故障后仍能继续跟踪。传统的级联控制架构将跟踪、分配与限幅分层处理，故障后各层难以协调，导致路径偏离，因此需要一种统一处理约束与故障重构的控制方法。

Method: 在单推进器命令层面构建单个二次规划（QP），同时求解轨迹跟踪、推力分配以及每个推进器的推力与速率限制；故障重构通过外部检测器输出的每个推进器一个二值标志进入该QP。将该统一架构与作者自研的级联基线架构进行对比。

Result: 现场实验测得：10分钟定点保持的均方根位置误差为1.6 cm；以0.6 m/s跟踪10 m方形轨迹的误差为4.3 cm。在0.5 m/s下失去前推进器使误差增至11.6 cm，失去右舷侧推进器增至11.5 cm；同时失去两个推进器时，艇仍能以0.4 m/s跟踪方形轨迹，均方根位置误差为1.25 m。与级联基线相比，统一架构在标称方形轨迹上跟踪精度相当（同为几厘米），且定点保持更紧、所需推力约为其一半；故障后两者出现分化，统一控制器保持在参考路径24 cm以内，而级联架构则偏离路径。

Conclusion: 将跟踪、推力分配与推进器力/速率约束统一到单个二次规划、并以二值故障标志进行重构的架构，能够在真实水上演出中实现厘米级跟踪精度，并在推进器故障后显著优于级联基线，具备工程可部署性与容错能力。

Abstract: Choreographed aquatic performances require small autonomous surface vehicles to track precise paths under per-thruster force and rate limits, including after thruster failures. We report a deployed system in which trajectory tracking, thrust allocation, and the per-thruster force and rate limits are resolved in a single quadratic program over the per-thruster commands, with fault reconfiguration entering through one binary flag per thruster from an external detector. The system has driven a fleet in live performances on Lake Zürich and at the Time Space Existence 2025 exhibition in Venice. The field campaign measures 1.6 cm root mean square position error in a 10-minute hold and 4.3 cm over a 10 m square at 0.6 m/s. At 0.5 m/s, losing the front thruster increases the error to 11.6 cm, while losing the starboard-side thruster increases it to 11.5 cm. Losing two thrusters simultaneously leaves the craft tracking a 0.4 m/s square with a root mean square position error of 1.25 m. Compared to our own cascaded baseline, the unified formulation tracks the nominal square to the same few centimeters and holds station more tightly with roughly half the thruster force. The architectures separate after a thruster failure, where the unified controller stays within 24 cm of the reference path while the cascade leaves it.

</details>


### [91] [PlantShade: Predicting Plant Shadows for Lighting-Aware Robotic Agricultural Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21059)
*Longchao Da,Xiaoou Liu,Xingjian Li,Lirong Xiang,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对植物遮阴模拟的重要性和复杂性，构建了涵盖四种作物的植物生长与阴影数据集，并提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，引入时间条件，支持未见植物的真实阴影生成及下游农业机器人任务，并通过定量与定性评估验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生长与农业生产是国家可持续发展和民生的重要基础，前沿人工智能为科学农业和提升作物生产力提供了潜力。遮阴是影响植物生长的关键因素，但植物阴影模拟具有重要性和内在复杂性。作者希望推进该领域研究并促进更广泛的社会效益。

Method: 主要包含两项贡献：第一，构建一个全面的植物生长与阴影数据集，覆盖大豆、番茄、甜菜和草莓四种植物，包含俯视视角，并设置沿圆形轨迹的补充光源，在多个生长阶段产生动态阴影，具有多样的观测复杂性；第二，提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，可为未见植物生成真实阴影，支持感知、光照控制和视角规划等下游机器人任务，并通过引入时间条件实现不同时间阶段的灵活阴影模拟。

Result: 作者开展了定量和定性评估以衡量模型性能。摘要未给出具体指标或数值结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为植物感知的阴影建模提供了基础性研究，对更广泛的农业和机器人应用具有意义。

Abstract: Plant growth and agricultural production form the foundation of a country's sustainable development and directly impact human livelihoods. Recent advances in frontier artificial intelligence have enabled scientific agriculture with strong potential to improve crop productivity. In this paper, we identify the importance and inherent complexity of plant shade simulation, as shading is a critical factor influencing plant growth. To advance this field and promote broader societal benefits, we focus on two main contributions. First, we introduce a comprehensive plant growth and shade dataset covering four plant species, including soybean, tomato, sugarbeet, and strawberry. The dataset includes top-down viewpoints with a supplementary light along a circular trajectory, casting dynamic shadows across multiple growth stages and diverse observation complexities. Second, we propose generative shade simulation based on diffusion models, enabling realistic shade generation for unseen plants and supporting downstream robotic tasks such as perception, lighting control, and view planning. The model incorporates temporal conditioning to facilitate flexible shade simulation across different time stages. We conduct both quantitative and qualitative evaluations to assess model performance. This work provides a foundational study for plant-aware shade modeling and has meaningful implications for broader agricultural and robotic applications.

</details>


### [92] [Design of Adaptive PID Controller Based On Asynchronous Advantage Actor Critic Learning Method for QuadCopter Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21082)
*Ali Jokar,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将A3C强化学习与PID控制相结合的四旋翼姿态与轨迹跟踪框架，利用并行智能体动态优化PID参数，并借助系统辨识模块预测状态，仿真表明其优于A2C且收敛更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼具有非线性强、对扰动敏感等特点，传统PID控制器难以应对复杂控制需求，因此需要引入更具适应性的智能控制方法。

Method: 构建A3C与PID结合的控制器：A3C通过多个并行智能体及神经网络动态整定PID参数；同时设置系统辨识模块，对互补系统进行状态预测，以辅助获得最优控制策略。

Result: 与标准A2C模型对比，二者在仿真中均能实现较精确的跟踪；但A3C控制器在损失函数上收敛更充分，奖励曲线和损失曲线均显示出更好的参数优化效果。

Conclusion: A3C与PID融合的方法提升了四旋翼控制性能，表明将强化学习与传统控制结合可有效增强系统适应性。

Abstract: Quadcopters offer great utility in many applications, but their nonlinear nature and disturbance sensitivity present great control challenges. Basic PID controllers are generally not sophisticated enough to cope with these complexities. This paper suggests a control system that integrates the Asynchronous Advantage Actor-Critic (A3C) algorithm with a PID controller for quadcopter attitude and trajectory tracking. The A3C controller uses parallel agents to optimize PID parameters dynamically using a neural network. A system identification module for the complementary system makes predictions about system states for optimal control policy. The proposed framework was compared with a standard actor-critic (A2C) model. Simulation results verify that they both track accurately. However, the A3C-based controller converges much more for the loss function, as evidenced by reward figures and loss curves, demonstrating better parameter optimization. This shows that A3C-based approach results in improved performance for the control of quadcopter, effectively integrating reinforcement learning and traditional control to achieve higher adaptability.

</details>


### [93] [Dynamic Modeling and LQR Control of a Single Coaxial Drone with 2DOF Thrust Vectoring Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21099)
*Ali Jokar,Amin Talaeizadeh,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带两自由度摆式机构的共轴无人机，实现主动推力矢量，以较低机械复杂度解决滚转/俯仰欠驱动问题；建立完整拉格朗日动力学模型，设计LQR控制器，并用EKF融合GPS、气压计和IMU进行状态估计，仿真验证其节能与快速飞行潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轴旋翼无人机因能效高、体积小受到关注，但滚转和俯仰控制存在固有欠驱动问题；斜盘机构虽可解决该限制，却带来更高的机械复杂度，因此需要一种机械结构更简单的替代方案。

Method: 设计两自由度摆机构实现主动推力矢量；建立不忽略机体、舵机臂和电机组件惯性贡献的完整拉格朗日动力学模型；在悬停平衡点附近线性化并设计LQR控制器；开展考虑执行器动态和传感器噪声的高保真仿真；采用EKF融合GPS、气压计和IMU进行状态估计。

Result: 高保真仿真验证了所提架构的可行性，EKF能够高精度融合GPS、气压计和IMU估计状态，结果证明该方法在节能、快速共轴无人机方面具有潜力和可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究为共轴无人机提供了一种机械复杂度较低的主动推力矢量方案，并通过动力学建模、LQR控制和EKF状态估计验证了其可行性与应用潜力。

Abstract: Coaxial rotor drones have generated considerable interest because of energy efficiency and small size, but they are afflicted with inherent underactuation for roll and pitch control, although systems like swashplates have circumvented this limitation at the cost of greater mechanical complexity. This work presents a novel coaxial drone supplemented by a two-degreesof-freedom pendulum mechanism for active thrust vectoring that offers a less mechanically complicated alternative. We develop a comprehensive Lagrangian dynamic model that does not ignore the inertial contributions of all the components, including body, servo arms, and motor assembly. A Linear Quadratic Regulator(LQR) is designed based on the linearized dynamics around the hover equilibrium. High-fidelity simulations taking actuator dynamics and sensor noise into account validate the proposed architecture. An Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) blends GPS, barometer, and IMU estimates with high accuracy for state estimation. The findings verify the potential and reliability of this approach for power-saving, rapid coaxial UAVs.

</details>


### [94] [Dynamics-Induced Commitment in Learning-Based Robotic Penalty Kicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21100)
*Ruize Geng,Hao E. Zhang,Yisen Li,Yikai Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究分层人形-四足点球系统中策略信息与身体执行能力的耦合，提出DIC-Map刻画动态诱导承诺；承诺约在触球前0.29 s，用估计器替换可把扑救率从0.240提高到0.472。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器博弈学习不仅受策略信息约束，也受身体是否仍能执行某动作约束；作者希望理解游戏级自博弈策略与固定全身控制器之间的耦合，尤其是终止替代方案何时不可逆丧失，以及响应方如何据此选择承诺或延迟。

Method: 构建分层人形-四足罚球系统：游戏级自博弈策略指挥固定的足球全身控制器（S-WBC）；人形射门技能由自采集动捕初始化，四足扑救技能用强化学习训练。提出DIC-Map（dynamics-induced commitment mapping）：基于身体状态估计继续执行能力，识别某终止替代方案首次持续丧失，并检验剩余交互能否约化为零和博弈。对对称终止替代，给出由响应方延迟价值决定的最优策略集中度闭式界；当响应方经由估计器行动时，证明相等响应值会消除直接终端分配梯度，仅留下估计器中介的一阶学习通道。由于覆盖项仅为观测代理，采用后验分析进行均衡比较。

Result: 实验将承诺定位在接触前约0.29 s；仅改变球速即可改变延迟覆盖。四种响应策略下，替换估计器使扑救率由0.240升至0.472；而以等待获得相近读取准确率增益时仅升至0.246。

Conclusion: 身体动力学造成的承诺约束是机器人博弈学习的关键限制；估计器中介的学习通道比单纯提高读取准确率更能提升响应表现。DIC-Map为身体受限下的博弈约化、承诺识别与策略集中度分析提供了可操作框架。

Abstract: Learning in robotic games is constrained not only by strategic information but also by what the body can still execute. We study this coupling in a hierarchical humanoid-quadruped penalty system in which game-level self-play policies command fixed soccer whole-body controllers (S-WBCs). The humanoid shooting skill is initialized from self-collected motion-capture data, whereas the quadruped saving skill is learned by reinforcement learning. We introduce dynamics-induced commitment mapping (DIC-Map), a body-grounded analysis that estimates continuation capability, identifies the first persistent loss of a terminal alternative, and tests whether the remaining interaction admits a reduced zero-sum game. For symmetric terminal alternatives, the reduced game yields a closed-form bound on optimal strategy concentration determined by the responder's value of deferring. We further show that, when the responder acts through an estimator, equal response values eliminate the direct terminal-allocation gradient and leave an estimator-mediated first-order learning channel. Experiments locate commitment about 0.29 s before contact, and changing only ball speed shifts deferral coverage. Across four responder policies, replacing the estimator raises save rate from 0.240 to 0.472, whereas a comparable gain in read accuracy obtained by waiting raises it only to 0.246. Posterior analysis is used for the equilibrium comparison because the available coverage terms are observational proxies. Project website: https://chris-ruizegeng.github.io/penaltykick/

</details>


### [95] [Learning Scene-Aware Humanoid Locomotion through 3D Clutter from Immersive Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21107)
*Beichen Wang,Tong Xu,Daniel Kosukhin,Yuen-Hei Yeung,Yuanjie Lu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MTC：基于示教学习、面向密集杂乱三维环境的场景感知人形机器人运动框架，通过VR采集人类动作、场景感知重定向生成无碰撞参考轨迹，并训练可在Unitree G1上部署的运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习人类动作的方法已实现人形机器人在无障碍空间中的高动态技能，但在密集杂乱环境中的穿越仍研究不足。此类空间具有三维几何约束，要求全身运动与场景几何紧密耦合以实现避障，现有方法难以直接适用。

Method: 提出MTC框架：利用程序化生成的虚拟现实环境进行沉浸式数据采集，规避高昂的物理场景搭建成本；设计场景感知运动重定向算法，将人类示教动作转换为人形机器人轨迹，并严格约束机器人与场景间的间隙以保证无碰撞穿越；基于这些参考轨迹训练场景感知运动策略，并部署到Unitree G1人形机器人。

Result: 在作者提出的多障碍穿越基准MTC-Challenge上评估，策略在多种场景中取得70.2%的无碰撞通过率，能够通过爬行穿越低净空通道、挤过狭窄缝隙等多样化全身技能成功穿越复杂环境。

Conclusion: MTC表明，结合程序化VR示教采集、场景感知运动重定向与策略学习，可有效实现人形机器人在密集杂乱环境中的场景感知全身运动与无碰撞穿越，为人形机器人复杂三维空间导航提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While learning from human motions has enabled highly dynamic humanoid skills such as dancing and martial arts in obstacle-free space, traversal through densely cluttered environments remains underexplored. These spaces are three-dimensional and geometrically constrained, requiring scene-aware locomotion that tightly couples whole-body motion with scene geometry for obstacle avoidance. To address these challenges, we present Moving Through Clutter (MTC), a learning-from-demonstration framework for scene-aware humanoid locomotion. To bypass costly physical scene construction, MTC uses procedurally generated Virtual Reality environments for immersive data collection. To transform these human motions into training-ready humanoid motions, we propose a scene-aware motion retargeting algorithm that converts human demonstrations into humanoid trajectories while strictly enforcing robot-scene clearance to guarantee collision-free traversal. These reference trajectories are then used to train a scene-aware locomotion policy that deploys on a Unitree G1 humanoid. Evaluated on our proposed MTC-Challenge for multi-obstacle traversal, the policy demonstrates a 70.2% collision-free rate across diverse scenarios, successfully traversing complex environments through diverse whole-body skills, including crawling through low-clearance passages and squeezing through narrow gaps.

</details>


### [96] [Demonstration Synthesis from a Single Scan via Gaussian Splatting for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21112)
*Beichen Wang,Yuen-Hei Yeung,V. R. Sridhar Devarakonda,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianFactory：仅需一次视频扫描即可批量生成高保真演示数据，无需物理引擎参与生成循环；在仿真与真实UR10e平台上，纯用合成演示训练的扩散策略成功率分别达95.1%和84.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略训练依赖与目标环境高度匹配的大量演示，但重新采集成本高昂。现有演示合成方法虽能降本，仍受限于人工投入大、视觉保真度不足或对物理仿真器依赖过重。

Method: 提出GaussianFactory高保真数据引擎：以单次视频扫描为唯一人工输入，生成循环中不使用物理引擎。将场景重建为可编辑的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）副本，并采样场景所支持的对象组合任务；对每个任务在几何重建上纯运动学地规划抓取与轨迹，渲染与目标环境视觉匹配的逼真演示。物理动力学仅在接触力决定结果处引入——抓取形成阶段，通过一次在交互数据集上预训练的学习接触模型处理。

Result: 实现端到端“扫描到部署”流程，在两种设置中验证：以仿真场景代表真实世界以保证可复现性，以及含物理UR10e机器人的真实工作空间。仅用合成演示训练的标准扩散策略分别达到95.1%和84.2%的成功率。

Conclusion: GaussianFactory证明可仅凭单次扫描、无需物理引擎批量生产高保真且视觉对齐目标环境的演示，并支撑视觉运动策略从扫描到真实部署，显著降低演示采集成本。

Abstract: Training a visuomotor policy calls for abundant demonstrations that closely match the target environment, yet collecting them anew remains expensive. Existing demonstration synthesis methods reduce this cost but remain constrained by high manual effort, limited visual fidelity, or heavy reliance on physics simulators. This paper introduces GaussianFactory, a high-fidelity data engine that mass-produces demonstrations with a single video scan as its only human input and no physics engine in the generation loop. Specifically, GaussianFactory reconstructs the scene as an editable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) replica and samples from the object-combination tasks the scene affords. For each task, it plans grasps and trajectories purely kinematically on the geometric reconstruction, rendering photorealistic demonstrations that visually match the target environment. Physical dynamics enter the pipeline only where contact force interactions dictate the outcome---during grasp formation, via a learned contact model pretrained once on an interaction dataset. To evaluate the downstream utility of the synthesized demonstrations, we implement an end-to-end scan-to-deployment workflow in two setups: a simulated scene that stands in for the real world to enable reproducibility, and a real-world workspace with a physical UR10e robot. In each setup, a standard diffusion policy trained solely on the synthesized demonstrations achieves 95.1% and 84.2% success rates, respectively.

</details>


### [97] [Noctif3R: Feed-Forward Monocular Real-Time SLAM for Photon-Limited Scenes on Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21114)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aditya Uday Abhang,Kevin Biju Mathew,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向暗光环境下单目机器人定位的实时、低功耗、低信噪比交叉约束问题，现有离线低光重建精度高但太慢，实时单目SLAM在低SNR下退化或失效。作者提出SYS：低光前馈点图前端+显式匹配门控，并给出Jetson AGX Orin嵌入式执行路径与位姿求解修复。SYS在可追踪场景中误差最低且不输出无信息轨迹，但在极暗条件下覆盖最窄；嵌入式优化带来吞吐提升、显存与能耗下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在极暗环境中需仅凭单个RGB相机、在接近传感器噪声的逐像素信号下，于功耗受限的机载计算机上实时定位。各单项约束已有成熟方案，但三者交叉处尚无可行方案：离线低光重建可恢复-4 dB以下结构但远不能实时；机器人可搭载的实时单目系统（DROID-SLAM、DPV-SLAM等）在低信噪比下性能下降或失败。作者还定量测量了这些系统在最低暗度层级上的失败模式，说明问题真实存在且严重。

Method: 提出SYS单目流水线：基于低光前馈点图（pointmap）前端，并引入显式匹配门控（match gate），以决定是否输出可信轨迹。为满足机载实时性，设计面向Jetson AGX Orin的嵌入式执行路径：地图、关键帧和后端在384像素分辨率运行，跟踪在256像素运行，并对逐帧位姿求解做两处修复。评估使用校准的、比特精确可复现的噪声阶梯，重标注的真实暗光曝光数据，以及新采集的Boston Dynamics Spot暗室视频阶梯。

Result: 在九个最低暗度层级上，DROID-SLAM全部返回无相机运动信息的全长度轨迹；VGGT-SLAM和CUT3R为8个，pi^3为7个，DPV-SLAM为4个。SYS返回3条被跟踪轨迹且无无信息轨迹；在可跟踪条件下误差最低，为无信息上限的24–47%，而最强基线为56–73%，但覆盖范围最窄。真实机器人视频中86.5%帧全黑时，作者所有配置在明亮起始段后停止，而DROID-SLAM和DPV-SLAM对全部1178帧均输出位姿。嵌入式优化形成可复现的Pareto改进：场景一吞吐1.28倍且误差0.964倍，场景二吞吐1.42倍且误差0.68倍，峰值GPU显存降低47%，每个位姿能耗降低29%。

Conclusion: 现有实时单目SLAM与离线低光重建均无法满足暗光、实时、低功耗的交叉需求，且会在极低SNR下输出无信息位姿。SYS通过低光点图前端、匹配门控与嵌入式执行优化，在可跟踪场景中实现更低误差与更可信的轨迹输出，并显著改善吞吐、显存和能耗；代价是极暗条件下覆盖率更窄。该工作为暗光机器人单目定位提供了可复现评测基准与机载可行方案。

Abstract: Robots carrying out tasks in dark environments need to localize from a single RGB camera, in light so low that the per-pixel signal approaches the sensor's own noise, on a power-constrained onboard computer, in real time. Each of these constraints has matured pipelines, but the intersection does not. Offline low-light reconstruction now recovers structure below -4 dB but is far too slow to run in real time, while the real-time monocular systems a robot can actually carry (DROID-SLAM, DPV-SLAM, etc.) degrade or fail when SNR gets low. We measured how they fail: across the nine lowest darkness levels of our scenes, DROID-SLAM returns a full-length trajectory carrying no information about the camera's motion on all nine, VGGT-SLAM and CUT3R on eight, pi^3 on seven, and DPV-SLAM on four. We present SYS, a monocular pipeline built on a low-light feed-forward pointmap front end with an explicit match gate, which returns three tracked trajectories and no uninformative ones, at the lowest error of any method where it tracks (24-47% of the no-information ceiling against 56-73% for the strongest baseline), and at the narrowest coverage. On a real robot video take in which 86.5% of delivered frames are entirely black, every configuration of ours stops after the lit beginning, while DROID-SLAM and DPV-SLAM each emit a pose for all 1178 frames. Our method contribution is an embedded execution path for the Jetson AGX Orin: running the map, keyframes and backend at 384 pixels with tracking at 256, together with two fixes to the per-frame pose solve, is a replicated Pareto improvement, 1.28x throughput at 0.964x error on one scene and 1.42x at 0.68x on a second, with 47% less peak GPU memory and 29% less energy per pose. We evaluate on a calibrated, bit-exact regenerable noise ladder, on relabelled real-world dark exposures, and on a new dark-room video ladder recorded from a Boston Dynamics Spot robot.

</details>


### [98] [MetaPusher: Meta Learning and Planning for Nonprehensile Manipulation of Unseen Objects with Rapid Online Adaption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21122)
*Donghyung Lee,Seyedali Golestaneh,Jaskrit Singh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Athanasios Kapoutsis,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MetaPusher：一种元学习与自适应规划框架，用于在无对象先验交互条件下实现对未见物体的非抓取式操作，通过元学习动力学模型快速适应与自适应运动动力学规划器复用搜索树，实现无需独立数据采集阶段的"边操作边适应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作未见物体困难，因其动力学依赖摩擦、质量分布等无法仅从感知推断的潜在物理属性。跨对象的先验经验虽能提供初始估计，但该估计不确定，且在仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移中会进一步退化。通过交互逐步适应动力学可修正估计，但更新模型可能使已规划的轨迹失效。因此成功且高效的操作需要兼具快速动力学适应能力与能融合该演化的规划策略。

Method: 提出MetaPusher框架，包含两个耦合组件：（1）元学习的动力学模型，可在任务执行过程中从交互中快速适应；（2）自适应运动动力学规划器，通过复用并细化已有搜索树来更新长时程规划。二者耦合使得操作与适应可在无独立数据采集阶段的情况下同时进行。

Result: 在仿真及sim-to-real场景中对未见物体进行评估，与微调、主动学习方法、基于MPPI的控制以及强化学习策略进行对比。MetaPusher取得更低的预测误差，并将任务成功率提升最高达20%。

Conclusion: 将元学习动力学适应与基于搜索树复用的自适应运动动力学规划相结合，可有效支持未见物体的非抓取式操作，无需预先的针对性数据采集，在仿真与真实迁移场景中均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Manipulating previously unseen objects remains challenging, as their dynamics depend on latent physical properties, such as friction and mass distribution, that cannot be inferred from perception alone. Prior experience across objects can provide an initial estimate of unseen object dynamics, but this estimate remains uncertain and can degrade further during sim-to-real transfer. Adapting the dynamics through interaction can progressively refine the estimation, however, updating the model may invalidate the planned trajectory. Successful and efficient manipulation therefore requires both rapid dynamics adaptation and a planning strategy that can incorporate this evolution. In this work, we introduce MetaPusher, a meta-learning and adaptive planning framework for nonprehensile manipulation of unseen objects without prior object-specific interactions. A meta-learned dynamics model rapidly adapts from interactions during task execution, while an adaptive kinodynamic planner updates long-horizon plans by reusing and refining its existing search tree. This coupling enables manipulation and adaptation without a separate data collection phase. We evaluate MetaPusher on unseen objects in simulation and in sim-to-real scenarios, comparing against fine-tuning and active learning methods, MPPI-based control, and a reinforcement learning policy. It achieves lower prediction error and improves task success rate by up to 20%.

</details>


### [99] [SAGE: Safety-Aligned Gradient Enforcement for Human--Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21130)
*Yisen Li,Hao Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出安全对齐梯度强制（SAGE），通过SAIL与TALO分别缓解CBF安全投影导致的环境动作偏移，以及多智能体独立actor更新与团队级更新不一致的问题，在可解释决策树策略与CBF滤波架构下实现安全与任务成功的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体人机协作要求机器人决策可解释、可审计，同时物理交互中的执行动作必须满足安全约束。将可解释决策树策略与控制屏障函数（CBF）滤波结合是一种有前景的架构，但在多智能体强化学习中会引入两类学习错配：一是安全投影会改变实际作用于环境的动作；二是耦合提议图会使独立优化的actor更新与团队级更新不一致。

Method: 提出SAGE。其包含：1）SAIL（shield-annealed internalization layer，屏蔽退火内化层）：使用可微有限惩罚提议映射，同时保留精确CBF二次规划用于执行，保持约束法向敏感性，从而内化反复激活的安全约束；2）TALO（team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization，团队平均Lyapunov策略优化）：构建团队感知的更新参考，并施加Lyapunov半空间校正，以规范独立actor更新。

Result: 在两个类人机器人加人类伙伴的物理实验中验证了部署可行性。九种仿真场景中，SAGE达到71.0%成功率，每千环境步0.5个碰撞步。消融显示：直接CBF滤波使碰撞频率降低98.5%，但成功率从67.3%降至59.3%；SAIL使提议违反降低48.8%，提议-执行校正降低85.2%；TALO使更新一致性差距降低50.8%。

Conclusion: SAGE能够同时处理安全投影与团队更新错配，在保持CBF执行安全性的同时缓解直接滤波带来的成功率损失；SAIL与TALO分别有效降低提议违反、提议-执行偏差和更新一致性差距，表明该框架在多主体人机协作中兼顾安全性、可解释性与任务性能。

Abstract: Multi-party human-robot collaboration poses a dual challenge: robot decisions should remain interpretable and auditable, while executed actions must satisfy safety constraints during physical interaction. Combining explainable decision-tree policies with control-barrier-function (CBF) filtering provides a promising architecture but creates two learning mismatches in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Safety projection changes the action applied to the environment, while the coupled proposal graph can misalign independently optimized actor updates with a team-level update. We present safety-aligned gradient enforcement (SAGE) to address both mismatches. Its shield-annealed internalization layer (SAIL) uses a differentiable finite-penalty proposal map while retaining the exact CBF quadratic program for execution, preserving constraint-normal sensitivity to internalize repeatedly active safety constraints. Team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization (TALO) constructs a team-aware update reference and applies a Lyapunov half-space correction to regulate independent actor updates. Physical experiments with two humanoid robots and a human partner demonstrate deployment feasibility. Across nine simulation scenarios, SAGE achieves a 71.0% success rate with 0.5 collision steps per thousand environment steps. Ablations show that direct CBF filtering reduces collision frequency by 98.5% but decreases success from 67.3% to 59.3%. SAIL reduces proposal violation by 48.8% and proposal-execution correction by 85.2%, while TALO reduces the update-consistency gap by 50.8%.

</details>


### [100] [Diverse and Adaptable Arm Coordination for Octopus-Crawling via Diffusion-Based Uncertainty-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21138)
*Seung Hyun Kim,Heng-Sheng Chang,Kimia Kazemi,Prashant Mehta,Mattia Gazzola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散的不确定性感知优化算法DUO，为模拟肌肉驱动的CyberOctopus学习无演示爬行控制器，首次将扩散控制用于接触丰富仿真中的软体多臂机器人，通过协调多样性实现鲁棒适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 章鱼爬行启发了利用冗余的软体机器人，但为适应环境而发现并组织多样化的协调模式仍具挑战性。软体多臂机器人在接触丰富的仿真中，如何学习多样且可迁移的运动控制策略是关键问题。

Method: 提出扩散式不确定性感知优化（DUO）算法，为模拟的肌肉驱动CyberOctopus学习无演示的爬行控制器。该方法将多种运动行为嵌入共享控制分布，包含三项贡献：(i) 对称结构化策略表示，将径向等价控制器折叠到规范方向扇区；(ii) 在线黑盒优化策略DUO，用于发现并保留多样化协调模式；(iii) 控制编辑技术，无需重新训练即可使现有控制器适应新的执行器约束。

Result: 所提方法使模拟章鱼能够在动态物理约束下导航；嵌入共享分布的多样运动行为验证了学习到的协调多样性能够内在地促进鲁棒适应；控制编辑技术可在不重新训练的情况下适应新执行器约束。

Conclusion: 学习到的协调多样性使运动冗余成为软体多臂机器人适应的实用资源，表明扩散式控制结合多样性优化是软体多臂机器人运动控制的有效途径。

Abstract: Octopus crawling motivates soft robots that exploit redundancy, yet discovering and organizing diverse coordination modes for adaptation remains challenging. To address this, we introduce a Diffusion-based Uncertainty-aware Optimization (DUO) algorithm that learns demonstration-free crawling controllers for a simulated, muscle-actuated CyberOctopus. This work represents the first application of diffusion-based control to soft multi-arm robots in contact-rich simulations. By embedding a variety of locomotion behaviors within a shared control distribution, this approach enables the simulated octopus to navigate dynamic physical constraints, demonstrating that learned coordination diversity inherently facilitates robust adaptation. The main contributions include: (i) a symmetry-structured policy representation that folds radially equivalent controllers into a canonical directional sector, (ii) an online black-box optimization strategy, the DUO algorithm, that discovers and retains diverse coordination modes, and (iii) a control editing technique that adapts existing controllers to novel actuator constraints without retraining. These results show how learned coordination diversity makes motor abundance a practical resource for adaptation in soft multi-arm robots.

</details>


### [101] [Same World, Different Knowledge: When Isolated Audits Misjudge World-Model Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21155)
*Rui Min,Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Sofiane Lachab,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向世界模型的“信息接口审计”，区分保真度缺口与可用性缺口，并通过固定权重干预评估预测误差、输入依赖性与闭环收益；在四旋翼MPC中表明，修复方案优劣取决于部署时共享的信息路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在孤立输入故障下表现良好的修复，当多个部署模块共享故障信息时可能反而更差；因此需要从信息接口角度审计世界模型，厘清“精确输入退化为估计”与“输入缺失”两类问题，并评估修复对闭环控制的实际影响。

Method: 提出信息接口审计框架，区分保真度缺口（fidelity gaps）与可用性缺口（availability gaps）；采用固定权重干预，测量预测误差、输入依赖性及成对闭环收益，并纳入重建过程引入的依赖关系。在仿真四旋翼模型预测控制中，比较物理锚定模型、不确定性训练、风重建等修复方式。

Result: 耦合且符号相反的10%质量/推力标定误差使物理锚定模型成功率从69%降至8%，不确定性训练可恢复至65%。风重建能恢复控制收益，但继承标定依赖性。对于正标定偏移，仅重建损坏时不确定性训练的重建更优，而共享损坏时基线更优。加速度诊断揭示重建偏差与名义模型误差之间存在补偿，扰动观测器中亦观察到类似现象。
