Extensive experiments across five agent benchmarks demonstrate that GraphSkillEvo consistently outperforms the strong skill optimization baseline SkillOpt, improving average accuracy by 4.01% on GPT-5.4-nano and 1.76% on GPT-5.4. Our code is available at https://github.com/ruisun7/GraphSkillEvo.

</details>


### [237] [Bilevel Optimization of Topology and Hyperparameters (BOTH)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21758)
*Suryanarayanan Manoj Sanu,Miguel Anibal Bessa,Alejandro Marcos Aragón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用自动微分对拓扑优化（TO）本身求导以获得“超梯度”，从而在主要优化过程中联合调节TO的超参数，避免依赖人工试错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化虽能自动化生成设计原型，但实际依赖大量“魔法数”超参数，其取值显著影响结果；调参通常需要深入的问题知识与大量试错。代理辅助超参数优化又受限于必须严格限制超参数数量。

Method: 对拓扑优化过程本身进行自动微分，计算关于TO超参数的“超梯度”，并与主要设计优化交替或联合更新；关键发现是只需评估一到两步TO即可提供足够信息，从而以截断展开方式高效计算超梯度。

Result: 在应力约束问题与柔度问题上验证了方法；柔度问题采用密度场的神经参数化。方法可扩展至数千个超参数，计算开销仅相当于数次标准TO运行，且一至两步TO评估已足够有效。

Conclusion: 该工作将超参数调优转化为可微优化问题，显著提升TO自动化程度，减少对手工“魔法数”调参的依赖，并为大规模超参数联合优化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Topology optimization (TO) represents a significant step towards automating the design process: given a working simulation, TO can produce a viable prototype at the press of a button by differentiating the simulation and iteratively improving the design. In practice, however, TO is riddled with ``magic numbers''---hyperparameters whose tuning significantly affects the outcome. Finding the right values typically requires not only deep problem-specific knowledge but also extensive trial-and-error. While practitioners can use surrogate-assisted hyperparameter optimization as an alternative, this approach requires strictly limiting the number of hyperparameters through careful problem formulation. Here, we propose differentiating TO itself using automatic differentiation. This yields ``hypergradients'' that allow us to tune these hyperparameters in tandem with the primary optimization. We show that evaluating just one or two steps of TO is sufficiently informative and that the method scales favorably to thousands of hyperparameters at an expense comparable to only a few standard TO runs. We demonstrate this approach on stress-constrained and compliance problems, with the latter utilizing a neural parameterization of the density field.

</details>


### [238] [RegKT: Interpretable and Robust Deep Knowledge Tracing With IRT-Regularizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21791)
*Samuel Girard,Juan D. Pinto,Jill-Jênn Vie,Amel Bouzeghoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的正则化技术，旨在同时提升基于深度学习的知识追踪模型的鲁棒性（缓解过拟合）与可解释性，使其更适用于真实教育场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型不断发展，知识追踪（Knowledge Tracing）模型的准确率不断提高，但这一提升是以可解释性下降为代价的；而可解释性对教育实践者采纳新方法至关重要。此外，深度学习模型容易过拟合，在教育应用中常见的小样本数据集上尤为突出。

Method: 提出一种新颖的正则化（regularization）技术，用于增强深度学习知识追踪模型的鲁棒性，并同时提升其可解释性，从可解释性与过拟合两个维度共同加以应对。

Result: 摘要为方法性概述，未给出具体数据集、基线对比或量化指标；仅声明该方法同时应对可解释性与过拟合问题，使模型在真实教育应用中更具可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持深度学习知识追踪模型能力的同时改善了鲁棒性与可解释性，从而提升了此类模型在真实教育场景中的实用性与可采纳性。

Abstract: As deep learning models continue to advance, knowledge tracing models have achieved higher accuracy. However, these gains come at the cost of reduced interpretability, which is crucial for practitioners in educational settings to adopt new methodologies. Additionally, deep learning models are prone to overfitting, particularly when dealing with the small datasets that are common in educational applications. In this paper, we propose a novel regularization technique designed to enhance the robustness of deep-learning-based knowledge tracing models, while simultaneously improving their interpretability. Our method addresses both the interpretability and overfitting challenges, making it more feasible for real-world educational applications.

</details>


### [239] [Matrix AdaGrad: Row-wise and Column-wise Adaptive Subgradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21815)
*Wenpeng Zhang,Runsheng Yu,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对矩阵值参数的在线镜像下降（OMD）通用框架，通过引入行/列方向的自适应近端函数，推导出行向与列向的矩阵 AdaGrad（Row-AdaGrad / Column-AdaGrad），并给出遗憾界证明与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdaGrad、Adam 等自适应优化方法虽广泛用于神经网络训练，但其自适应缩放主要面向向量参数设计，未显式利用矩阵结构。现有矩阵感知优化器虽展示出结构化优化的优势，却缺乏类似 AdaGrad 那样可用于推导矩阵自适应性的通用理论框架。

Method: 构建带自适应近端函数的矩阵值参数在线镜像下降（OMD）框架，通过在线遗憾最小化来推导矩阵感知自适应优化。引入行向与列向矩阵近端函数，并分析由此产生的遗憾权衡，进而导出 Row-wise Matrix AdaGrad（Row-AdaGrad）与 Column-wise Matrix AdaGrad（Column-AdaGrad），其自适应缩放由累积的行向或列向梯度范数决定。

Result: 建立了遗憾保证，并证明在结构化梯度下，这些矩阵感知界可严格紧于逐元素 AdaGrad 的界。在矩阵分解与深度神经网络训练实验中，验证了将自适应缩放与矩阵结构对齐的收益，包括在更大学习率和更深网络下优化稳定性与可训练性的提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了与 AdaGrad 理论地位相当的矩阵感知自适应优化通用框架，表明按行/列结构进行自适应缩放可带来更紧的理论界与更优的优化稳定性，为矩阵参数优化提供了原则性方法。

Abstract: Adaptive optimization methods such as AdaGrad and Adam are widely used in modern neural-network training, but their adaptive scaling is primarily designed for vector-valued parameters and does not explicitly exploit matrix structure. Recent matrix-aware optimizers demonstrate the benefits of structured optimization, yet a general theoretical framework for deriving matrix-aware adaptivity comparable to that of AdaGrad remains lacking. In this work, we develop a general Online Mirror Descent framework with adaptive proximal functions for matrix-valued parameters, providing a principled approach to deriving matrix-aware adaptive optimization through online regret minimization. By introducing row-wise and column-wise matrix proximal functions and analyzing the resulting regret trade-off, we derive Row-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Row-AdaGrad) and Column-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Column-AdaGrad), with adaptive scaling determined by the accumulated row-wise or column-wise gradient norms. We establish regret guarantees and show that these matrix-aware bounds can be strictly tighter than those of entry-wise AdaGrad under structured gradients. Experiments on matrix factorization and deep neural-network training further demonstrate the benefits of aligning adaptive scaling with matrix structure, including improved optimization stability and trainability at larger learning rates and greater network depths.

</details>


### [240] [Federated Deep Clustering Networks for High-Dimensional and Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21829)
*Morris Stallmann,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Marcin Pietrasik,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedDCN，将深度聚类网络推广到联邦场景，同时优化重构损失与聚类损失，并通过合成数据增强与几何正则化提升非独立同分布数据下的鲁棒性与潜空间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据聚类是无监督学习的基础任务，集中式场景下常用深度聚类方法。联邦学习中数据分散且私有，深度聚类研究较少；现有联邦深度聚类方法在客户端数据非独立同分布（non-IID）时性能不可靠。

Method: 将深度聚类网络（Deep Clustering Networks）推广到联邦场景，提出 FedDCN。该方法同时优化重构损失和聚类损失；为在 non-IID 数据下保证鲁棒性和潜空间对齐，FedDCN 生成合成数据增强，并在学习目标中引入用于潜空间对齐的几何正则化项。

Result: 通过实验评估，在 IID 与 non-IID 假设下均验证了方法的有效性，并据此指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: FedDCN 通过重构-聚类联合优化、合成数据增强与几何正则化，在联邦高维数据聚类中实现了对 IID 及 non-IID 分布的鲁棒性能，为该方向后续研究提供了基础与展望。

Abstract: Clustering high-dimensional data is a fundamental task in unsupervised machine learning with applications to a variety of domains. In the centralized data scenario, this task is commonly solved using deep clustering methods that utilize deep neural network architectures to learn clustering-friendly latent space representations. In Federated Learning, where data is distributed between clients and is private, deep clustering methods are less explored. In particular, recently introduced federated deep clustering methods, despite showing very promising performance, still fall short in reliably providing good performance if data across clients are non-identically-independently distributed. In this work, we introduce a generalization of Deep Clustering Networks to the federated scenario, named FedDCN, that simultaneously optimizes a reconstruction loss and a clustering loss. To ensure robustness and latent space alignment in non-identically-independently distributed data scenarios, FedDCN generates synthetic data augmentations, and its learning objective includes a geometric regularization for latent space alignment. Through experimental evaluation, the effectiveness of the approach under IID and non-IID assumptions is demonstrated, and future research directions are identified.

</details>


### [241] [The Weight Is Over - Interactive Diffusion on Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21849)
*Frieder Ganz,Maximilian Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向端侧扩散模型推理，提出嵌入翻译器、速度/质量/内存权衡的可复现扫描方法，以及交互式端侧图像生成编辑器，在较新GPU上实现亚秒级TTFI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧推理发展迅速，但主要集中在语言模型。扩散流水线内存消耗大、延迟敏感，且需要编排嵌入器、Transformer、解码器以及尚未像LLM推理循环那样标准化的后处理环节。因此需要在性能、质量和模型占用之间取得平衡，以覆盖尽可能多的真实客户端设备。

Method: 提出三项方法：1）嵌入翻译器（embedding translator），将小型文本编码器映射到大型编码器空间，以降低权重和延迟；2）面向扩散流水线的可复现扫描配方（sweep recipe），用于在速度/质量/内存三角中进行权衡导航；3）交互式端侧图像生成编辑器。

Result: 通过上述方法在性能、质量与模型占用之间进行权衡，并在较新GPU上实现亚秒级TTFI（time-to-first-image，首图生成时间）的交互式端侧图像生成体验。

Conclusion: 该工作将端侧扩散推理的优化重点从单纯压缩模型扩展到系统级流水线编排与权衡搜索，并通过嵌入空间映射和可复现扫描方法，在真实客户端设备上兼顾速度、质量与内存，实现交互式端侧图像生成。

Abstract: On-device inference is booming, but the momentum is almost all in language models. Diffusion pipelines are memory hungry, latency-sensitive, and require orchestrating an embedder, a transformer, a decoder, and often further postprocessing that is not as standardized as LLM inference loops are. We navigate the trade-off between performance, quality, and model footprint to reach as many client devices in the wild as possible. We make three contributions: an embedding translator that maps a small text encoder into a large encoder space to cut weight and latency; a reproducible sweep recipe for navigating the speed/quality/memory triangle in diffusion pipelines; and an interactive on-device image generation editor achieving sub-second TTFI on recent GPUs.

</details>


### [242] [Neural Cellular Automata Learn General Features in their Hidden Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21870)
*Etienne Guichard,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究神经细胞自动机（NCA）隐藏通道的内部动力学，提出一种将预训练教师隐藏状态注入学生模型以引导早期优化的迁移学习机制。在少样本与尺度变化的 MNIST 基准上，NCA 以约 9,800 参数优于同类循环/前馈架构，并揭示隐藏通道可解耦特征提取与分类共识、收敛为相互正交状态、捕获尺度不变的通用拓扑原语，从而支持跨未见类别的少样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度模型依赖过参数化取得泛化，但在少样本场景下易过拟合与记忆；NCA 虽参数高效，既有研究多关注其输出，对其内部隐藏通道的作用缺乏系统探索。

Method: 分析 NCA 隐藏通道的内部动态；设计迁移学习机制，将预训练教师模型的隐藏状态注入学生模型以指导早期优化；在少样本及尺度变化 MNIST 上评测，并进行机制分析（特征解耦、正交化、拓扑原语）。

Result: NCA 以约 9,800 参数优于可比的循环与前馈架构，泛化更强；隐藏通道吸收形态复杂度，将特征提取与均匀分类共识解耦，并收敛到相互正交状态；其捕获的是通用、尺度不变的拓扑原语而非类别特定模板，使学生模型可借助仅在数字 0–5 上训练的教师特征，在未见类别上取得强少样本性能。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态动力学可作为稳健、去中心化的计算基底，为参数高效迁移学习提供新路径。

Abstract: Modern deep learning models achieve impressive generalization through over-parameterization, but this paradigm often struggles with overfitting and memorization in few-shot regimes. Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) offer a highly parameter-efficient alternative, yet research has focused primarily on their output, leaving the role of their internal hidden channels largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the internal dynamics of NCA hidden channels and introduce a novel transfer-learning mechanism that injects a pretrained teacher's hidden states into a student model to guide early optimization. Evaluated on few-shot and scale-variant MNIST benchmarks, NCAs outperform comparable recurrent and feed-forward architectures, demonstrating superior generalization with a minimal parameter budget (~9,800 parameters). Mechanistic analysis reveals that the hidden channels decouple feature extraction from uniform classification consensus by absorbing morphological complexity and converging to mutually orthogonal states. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these hidden channels capture general, scale-invariant topological primitives rather than class-specific templates. This allows a student model to achieve strong few-shot performance on unseen classes using features transferred from a teacher trained only on a subset of digits (0-5). Our results highlight the potential of utilizing hidden-state dynamics as a robust, decentralized computational substrate for parameter-efficient transfer learning

</details>


### [243] [Geometric Mean Pooling for Equal-Weight Multiplicative Coarse-Graining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21876)
*Ang-Kun Wu,Fangdi Wen,Jingtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出符号化几何平均池化（GMP），以特征符号乘积与幅度几何平均组合替代平均/最大池化的加性与极值偏置；在合成任务中优于基线，在图像与分子任务中效果依赖表示、目标参数化与池化位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均池化偏向加性组合，最大池化偏向极值选择，二者均不天然适合需要等权乘性组合的任务；受量子多体物理中局部到全局组合的启发，作者希望引入一种保留联合符号信息与乘性尺度的无参数池化算子。

Method: 提出几何平均池化（GMP）：将特征符号的乘积与特征幅度的几何平均相结合，形成带符号的池化操作；该算子不引入可学习池化参数，并采用非重叠层次化GMP，以保持相应的全局乘性统计量。

Result: 在合成序列任务中，GMP比平均池化和最大池化更准确地恢复基于乘积的信号，并在一定乘性输入噪声下保持预测性能；在迭代粗粒化、图像分类和分子亲脂性回归中，GMP的有效性取决于表示形式、目标参数化以及局部与全局池化的放置方式。

Conclusion: GMP是一种互补的、依赖任务机制的归纳偏置，适用于等权乘性组合合理的场景，而非标准池化算子的通用替代方案。

Abstract: As an alternative to the additive and extremal biases of average and max pooling, we introduce Geometric Mean Pooling (GMP), a signed pooling operator that combines the product of feature signs with the geometric mean of feature magnitudes. Motivated by local-to-global composition in quantum many-body physics, GMP retains both joint sign information and a characteristic multiplicative scale without introducing learnable pooling parameters. We show that non-overlapping hierarchical GMP preserves the corresponding global multiplicative statistic and evaluate it on synthetic sequence tasks, iterative coarse-graining, image classification, and molecular lipophilicity regression. On the synthetic tasks, GMP recovers product-based signals more accurately than average and max pooling and maintains predictive performance under the tested levels of multiplicative input noise. On image and molecular data, however, its effectiveness depends on the representation, target parameterization, and placement of local and global pooling. These results position GMP as a complementary, regime-dependent inductive bias for tasks in which equal-weight multiplicative composition is plausible, rather than as a universal replacement for standard pooling operators.

</details>


### [244] [Detecting Pretraining Data in Large Language Models from a Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21888)
*Chenye Ke,Zirui Liu,Qi Liu,Yan Zhuang,Jintao Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出能量转移检测（ETD），用熵校正的倾斜边界替代仅基于似然的水平边界，从自由能转移视角检测大模型预训练数据，显著提升平均检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖似然检测预训练数据存在根本歧义：高似然既可能源于训练暴露，也可能源于模型强泛化能力。在预测损失与预测熵构成的联合空间中，仅似然检测器使用水平边界，容易将可预测的非成员误判为成员。

Method: 引入相对于预测熵评估预测损失的倾斜边界。理论分析表明，熵校正可在保留期望成员信号的同时降低其方差，从而提升成员与非成员之间的标准化可分性；并将均值—方差分析推广到熵均值差非零的一般情形。进一步发现该熵调整分数具有Helmholtz自由能解释，据此提出能量转移检测（ETD），从宏观残余自由能转移角度进行预训练数据检测。

Result: 大量实验表明，ETD取得最佳平均检测性能：平均AUROC最高提升3.5%，TPR@5%FPR最高提升5.1%，并在多种设置下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 通过熵校正与自由能视角重新刻画预训练数据检测，ETD有效缓解仅似然检测的误判问题，在平均检测指标和鲁棒性上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Detecting pretraining data in large language models is challenging because high likelihood can reflect either training exposure or strong generalization. In the joint space of prediction loss and predictive entropy, a likelihood-only detector uses a horizontal boundary and can mistake predictable non-members for members. Motivated by this, we introduce an inclined boundary that evaluates prediction loss relative to predictive entropy. Our analysis shows that entropy correction can preserve the expected membership signal while reducing its variance, thereby improving standardized member--non-member separation. We further extend the mean--variance analysis to the more general setting with a nonzero mean entropy gap. Interestingly, this entropy-adjusted score admits a Helmholtz free-energy interpretation, leading to Energy Transfer Detection (ETD), which views pretraining data detection from a macroscopic residual free-energy transfer perspective. Extensive experiments show that ETD achieves the best average detection performance, improving average AUROC by up to 3.5\% and TPR@5\%FPR by up to 5.1\%, while remaining robust across diverse settings.

</details>


### [245] [LLMs as Feature Engineers for Text-and-Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21894)
*Merwan Barlier,Blaz Skrlj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式框架，利用生成式LLM与抽取式LLM协同搜索可解释、受schema约束的类别特征，并通过将模型错误转化为自然语言反馈来加速表格预测任务的特征发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化文本用于表格预测时，现有特征工程难以兼顾可解释性与预测性能；同时，LLM驱动的特征搜索若缺乏引导，容易效率低下、难以针对具体预测失败进行优化。

Method: 框架包含三个角色：生成器LLM提出语义特征定义，抽取器LLM将定义实例化为特征，下游表格模型评估其预测性能。核心机制是把显式模型错误（如AUC排序反转）翻译为自然语言反馈，指导LLM解决特定预测失败，从而在特征空间中迭代搜索。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，错误驱动的迭代搜索相比无引导搜索最多加速特征发现3倍；生成特征与TF-IDF、稠密嵌入结合时表现出强多视图互补性，严格优于任何单一子集；所发现特征在SHAP重要性排序中占主导，并为每个预测提供透明的语义审计轨迹。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将LLM特征生成与下游模型错误反馈闭环结合，可同时提升特征发现效率、预测性能与实例级可解释性，为文本驱动的表格建模提供了一种可审计的自动化特征工程路径。

Abstract: We introduce an iterative framework that automates the extraction of interpretable, schema-bound categorical features from unstructured text for tabular prediction models. To navigate the feature space, a generator LLM proposes semantic definitions, a separate extractor LLM materializes the features, and a downstream tabular model evaluates their predictive performance. We optimize this search by translating explicit model errors, such as AUC ranking inversions, into natural-language feedback, steering the LLM to resolve specific predictive failures. Evaluated across three public datasets, this error-driven loop accelerates feature discovery by up to $3\times$ compared to unguided search. Empirically, the generated features demonstrate strong multi-view complementarity, strictly outperforming any subset when combined with TF-IDF and dense embeddings. Finally, the framework guarantees instance-level interpretability: the discovered features dominate SHAP importance rankings and provide a fully transparent, semantic audit trail for every prediction.

</details>


### [246] [ExpBoN: Exponential-Noise Best-of-$n$ for Efficient Test-Time LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21899)
*Yanxiao Liu,Sicheng Wan,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ExpBoN：一种基于指数噪声的 soft Best-of-n 采样方法，具备精确有限 n 分解、指数级收敛与 regret 理论分析，并集成到 guided speculative inference 形成 ExpGSI，在保持精度的同时显著降低 LLM 奖励引导对齐的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-n（BoN）是简单有效的推理时对齐方法，但硬最大化（hard maximization）只能在 reward 与 distribution shift 之间提供粗糙控制。Soft BoN（Verdun et al. 2025）虽更平滑并收敛到 KL 正则化奖励最大化的最优分布，仍需要新的机制与理论支撑；同时奖励引导的 LLM 对齐需要高效计算。

Method: 提出 ExpBoN，基于 exponential-noise report-noisy-max 机制的 soft BoN 方法。给出精确的有限 n 分解，从理论上分析其在 total variation、expected reward 以及两个方向的 KL divergence 上的指数级快速收敛，并研究收敛性与 regret 行为。进一步将 ExpBoN 集成到 guided speculative inference（GSI）框架，形成 ExpGSI，用于高效奖励引导的 LLM 对齐。

Result: ExpGSI 在保持可比精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。在 MATH500、MMLU-STEM、Minerva Math 上，使用 Qwen2.5-Math 与 Qwen3 模型家族：Qwen2.5-Math 在不同候选预算下估计计算量减少 14%–39%；Qwen3 在 n=16 时最高减少 45%。

Conclusion: 为指数噪声 BoN 与高效的测试时 LLM 对齐提供了理论与算法基础。ExpBoN/ExpGSI 兼顾收敛保证、regret 分析与推理效率，是 soft BoN 与奖励引导对齐的有效扩展。

Abstract: Best-of-$n$ (BoN) sampling is a simple yet effective inference-time alignment method, but hard maximization provides only coarse control over the trade-off between reward and distribution shift. Soft Best-of-$n$ (Verdun et al. 2025) provides smoother control and converges to the optimal distribution associated with KL-regularized reward maximization. In this paper, we introduce ExpBoN, an alternative soft BoN method based on the exponential-noise report-noisy-max mechanism. It admits an exact finite-$n$ decomposition, which yields exponentially fast convergence in total variation, expected reward, and both directions of KL divergence. We provide comprehensive theoretical analyses of its convergence and regret behavior. We further integrate ExpBoN into the guided speculative inference (GSI) framework (Geuter, Mroueh, and AlvarezMelis 2025), resulting in ExpGSI, for efficient reward-guided LLM alignment. ExpGSI yields substantial reductions in computational cost while maintaining comparable accuracy. Experiments on MATH500, MMLU-STEM, and Minerva Math with the Qwen2.5-Math and Qwen3 model families show that ExpGSI reduces estimated computation by $14\%$-$39\%$ across candidate budgets for Qwen2.5-Math and by up to $45\%$ at $n=16$ for Qwen3. Overall, our results provide a theoretical and algorithmic foundation for exponential-noise BoN and efficient test-time LLM alignment.

</details>


### [247] [Intervention Granularity Matters: Coherent Treatment Bundles in Counterfactual Simulation with Clinical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21906)
*Fangzhou Wang,Yixuan Yang,Camilla Balzarotti,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 反事实模拟中"一次干预"的粒度定义会实质性影响临床世界模型的响应：以完整治疗组合(bundle)编辑比单组件编辑带来更大的预测状态偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟需固定患者历史、改变治疗并读取预测响应，但前提是界定"一次干预"的边界。临床上干预以组合形式记录：对MIMIC-IV 945,707患者-小时的共现审计显示，诸如透析回路各参数等组件从不同时单独出现，因此单独改动某一组件会描述一个数据中从未出现的时刻。作者据此假设：干预被编辑的粒度会改变世界模型的响应。

Method: 采用Clin-JEPA——一个以逐小时治疗文本为条件、对患者轨迹建模的潜世界模型。在1,019次有创通气记录起始点，固定患者历史与其他治疗，比较两种编辑：编辑单个呼吸机设置，对比编辑某位近期轨迹最相似真实患者所记录的完整配置(bundle)。同时控制编辑对模型输入改变量的影响。

Result: 完整bundle比任何单一设置使预测的下一状态移动得更远，且该效应在全部五个设置上保持一致；在计入每次编辑对模型输入改变程度后，差异依然存在。

Conclusion: 干预粒度会实质性影响临床世界模型的响应：单组件编辑可能低估治疗敏感性，而bundle-aware编辑可为反事实治疗模拟提供证据支持更充分的基础。

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation with a clinical world model means fixing a patient's history, changing the treatment, and reading off the predicted response. Doing so requires deciding what counts as one intervention. In clinical settings, interventions are documented as bundles: a co-occurrence audit of 945,707 patient-hours from MIMIC-IV shows groups of components, such as every parameter of a dialysis circuit, that never appear apart, so an edit that changes one component on its own describes an hour that never occurs in the data. We hypothesize that the granularity at which an intervention is edited changes how a world model responds, and test this with Clin-JEPA, a latent world model of patient trajectories conditioned on hourly treatment text. At 1,019 documented onsets of invasive ventilation, we keep the patient's history and other treatments fixed and compare editing one ventilator setting with editing the complete configuration recorded for a real patient with the most similar recent trajectory. The complete bundle moves the predicted next state further than any single setting, consistently across all five settings, and the difference remains after accounting for how much each edit changes the model's input. Intervention granularity therefore materially affects the response of a clinical world model: single-component edits may understate treatment sensitivity, and bundle-aware editing may offer a better-supported basis for counterfactual treatment simulation.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond Kinematics: Benchmarking Simulation Fidelity for Muscle-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21909)
*Ayah G. Ahmad,Claire E. Borden,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统比较两种运动模仿强化学习(MIRL)流程——基于SCONE/HyFyDy与基于MuJoCo/MyoSim，发现二者运动学精度相近，但HyFyDy的肌肉激活更贴合实验EMG，故更适合肌肉骨骼建模，但两者均需进一步发展以实现GPU并行仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助设备设计与控制常以肌肉激活模式和代谢成本等指标作为优化目标，因此需要评估MIRL流程能否准确复现产生运动的底层神经肌肉行为。尽管已有研究证明两种流程能高保真复现人体运动学，但其对神经肌肉行为的捕捉能力尚不明确。

Method: 对两种最先进的MIRL流程进行系统性比较：一个基于SCONE/HyFyDy（强调通过详细肌腱建模实现生理真实性），另一个基于MuJoCo/MyoSim（侧重计算效率与可扩展的策略学习）。使用一套共同的人体运动捕捉与肌电(EMG)测量数据对两种流程进行评估。

Result: 两种流程产生的运动学均具有相对准确性且相似；但HyFyDy的肌肉激活与实验EMG更一致。肌肉激活的平均汇总(RMSE, r)值：HyFyDy为(0.164, 0.4)，MuJoCo为(0.344, 0.11)。

Conclusion: HyFyDy更先进的生理真实性使其当前更适合肌肉骨骼建模；但两者均需进一步发展，以将生理真实性引入GPU可并行化的仿真环境，从而推进机器人辅助设备的设计。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct a systematic comparison of two state-of-the-art motion-imitation reinforcement learning (MIRL) pipelines, one built on SCONE/HyFyDy and one built on MuJoCo/MyoSim. HyFyDy emphasizes physiological realism through detailed musculotendon modeling, while MuJoCo prioritizes computational efficiency and scalable policy learning. While recent work has demonstrated that both pipelines reproduce human kinematics with high fidelity, it remains unclear if they accurately capture the underlying neuromuscular behavior that produced the movement. This limitation is particularly important for robotic assistive-device design and control, where outcome measures such as muscle activation patterns and metabolic cost are often used as optimization targets. To conduct a systematic comparison, our work compares both pipelines using a common set of human motion-capture and electromyography (EMG) measurements. The results find that while both pipelines produce similar kinematics with relative accuracy, the muscle activations from HyFyDy are more aligned with the experimental EMG, as supported by the average pooled (RMSE, r) values for muscle activations from HyFyDy and MuJoCo: (0.164, 0.4) and (0.344, 0.11), respectively. While we conclude that the more advanced physiological realism of HyFyDy currently makes it more suitable for musculoskeletal modeling, both require further development to bring physiological realism to GPU-parallelizable simulation environments and advance robotic assistive device design.

</details>


### [249] [Kinks vs. Smoothness: Identifiability of Real Analytic nICA for Laplace-like Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21926)
*Isaac Manring,Kejun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明：在生成函数为实解析函数、且源概率密度函数的一阶导数仅有有限个不连续点的条件下，非线性独立成分分析（nICA）可辨识到平凡歧义；拉普拉斯分布是典型例子。证明利用源分布“折点”与实解析函数光滑性之间的对比。该结论可近似适用于使用 tanh、softplus、GELU 等标准激活函数的 Normalizing Flows 与 VAE，并在真实与合成数据上验证，CelebA 上可恢复可解释潜在因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习常将图像、金融时间序列等复杂数据解释为由隐藏独立因素生成；非线性 ICA 的核心挑战是恢复真实潜在因素而非其混淆版本。已有可辨识性结果通常依赖较强假设或特殊结构，限制其在实际训练流程中的适用性。

Method: 理论层面证明：当生成函数为实解析函数，且源概率密度函数的一阶导数只有有限个不连续点时，nICA 可实现精确恢复，仅存在平凡歧义。证明关键在于源分布中的“kink”（折点/不连续）与实解析函数光滑性之间的反差。由于实解析函数涵盖广泛生成机制，并可由使用标准激活函数（tanh、softplus、GELU）的 Normalizing Flows 或 VAE 近似，因此方法可最小改动地应用于现有训练流程。实验使用 Normalizing Flows 和 VAE，在真实与合成数据上评估可辨识性。

Result: 论文给出了在上述假设下的可辨识性证明，并以拉普拉斯分布作为满足条件的代表性源分布。实验在真实和合成数据上使用 Normalizing Flows 与 VAE，展示了其可辨识性质；在 CelebA 数据上恢复出若干可解释的潜在因子，这些因子控制数据集中的独特属性。

Conclusion: 该工作将非线性 ICA 的可辨识性扩展到实解析生成函数与具有有限一阶导数不连续点的源分布，利用非光滑源分布与光滑生成函数之间的对比实现精确恢复。结论对现有 Normalizing Flows 和 VAE 训练流程改动小，兼具理论保证与实证可行性，并支持可解释潜在因子发现。

Abstract: Many machine learning systems try to explain complex data - like images or financial time series - in terms of hidden, independent factors that generated them. Recovering the true underlying factors, rather than some scrambled version of them, is the central challenge of nonlinear Independent Component Analysis (nICA). We prove identifiability (exact recovery) up to trivial ambiguities for real analytic generating functions when source probability density functions have a finite number of discontinuities in the first derivative. The Laplace distribution is the most prominent example satisfying this assumption. Our proof relies on the contrast between kinks in the source distribution and the smoothness of real analytic functions. Real analytic functions comprise a broad class of generating mechanisms, and can be approximated with Normalizing Flows or Variational Autoencoders with standard activation functions (e.g., tanh, softplus, GELU), so our result applies with minimal changes to existing training pipelines. We perform experiments on real and synthetic data with both Normalizing Flows and Variational Auto-Encoders demonstrating their identifiability properties. In experiments on CelebA data we recover several interpretable latent factors controlling unique attributes across the dataset.

</details>


### [250] [Joint Remaining Useful Life Prediction and Capacity Estimation of Lithium-Ion Batteries Using Partial-Charging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21932)
*Khoa Tran,Ho-Si-Hung Nguyen,Phone Wai Yan Moe,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Thi-Hoang-Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种跨专家框架，仅用部分充电测量、无需历史满充容量，联合预测电池RUL并估计容量：RUL专家建模短期退化表示，容量专家建模长期统计表示，经FiLM条件调制融合。两公开数据集上RUL RMSE为143.69/161.10循环，容量误差为12.36/7.28 mAh；RUL精度领先，但容量精度存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合RUL预测与容量估计需同时表征电池的长期渐变退化与近期行为；实际应用中历史满充容量往往不可测，因此需在仅依赖部分充电测量、无历史满充容量输入的条件下实现联合预测。

Method: 构建跨专家框架。RUL专家：在30循环历史中采样10个循环，将标称10分钟片段输入预训练GRU编码器、2D-CNN与时间GRU，得到短期表示。容量专家：对连续10个循环的标称40分钟片段统计描述符，使用2D-CNN与Transformer，得到长期表示。采用FiLM模块以短期表示条件化长期表示，实现联合预测。训练分三阶段：监督自编码器预训练、专家独立预训练、冻结专家的融合训练。

Result: 在两个公开电池老化数据集上，参考配置平均RUL RMSE分别为143.69与161.10循环，容量误差分别为12.36与7.28 mAh。在Dataset I上，融合相较任一独立专家均降低两项平均误差。所提方法在两个数据集上取得对比方法中最低的RUL RMSE，但若干基线容量误差更低，表明RUL与容量精度之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 跨专家表示与FiLM条件融合可有效实现部分充电条件下的RUL预测和容量估计，并在RUL指标上达到领先水平；但容量估计精度相对部分基线仍有不足，说明两项任务联合优化时需进一步平衡精度权衡。

Abstract: Joint remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and capacity estimation require representations of both gradual degradation and recent battery behavior. This paper presents a cross-expert framework using partial-charging measurements without measured historical full-cycle capacity as an input. The RUL Expert encodes nominal 10-min segments from ten cycles sampled within a 30-cycle history using a pretrained gated recurrent unit (GRU) encoder, a two-dimensional convolutional neural network (2D-CNN), and a temporal GRU. The Capacity Expert processes statistical descriptors of nominal 40-min segments from ten consecutive cycles using a 2D-CNN and a Transformer. A feature-wise linear modulation module uses the short-term representation to condition the long-term representation for joint prediction. Training comprises supervised autoencoder pretraining, independent expert pretraining, and fusion training with frozen experts. On two public battery-aging datasets, the reference configuration achieves mean RUL root-mean-square errors of 143.69 and 161.10 cycles and capacity errors of 12.36 and 7.28mAh, respectively. On Dataset I, fusion reduces both mean errors relative to either standalone expert. The results demonstrate a trade-off between RUL and capacity accuracy: the proposed method attains the lowest reported RUL RMSE among the compared methods on both datasets, whereas several baselines yield lower capacity errors.

</details>


### [251] [Learning to Move Cities: Deep Meta-Models and Reinforcement Policies for Calibration and Control in Urban Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21945)
*Adewumi Augustine Adepitan,Christopher J. Haruna,Oluwasegun Adegoke,Ayooluwatomiwa Ajiboye,Oluwatobi Oluwasakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出共享潜在空间框架，将城市交通仿真器校准与强化学习控制统一：用组合式MLP自编码器学习低维交通动态表示，用于贝叶斯校准，并作为DQN状态表示优化动态交通分配，基准网络中系统总行程时间最多降低51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通网络优化同时面临高保真仿真器校准与实时运行控制难题；传统优化方法在大规模网络中易遇计算瓶颈，亟需一种能连接校准与控制、降低计算复杂度的统一表示。

Method: 构建组合式MLP自编码器，学习连接仿真器输入（起讫点需求、网络参数）与输出（行程时间、拥堵模式）的低维流形，以提升贝叶斯优化校准的样本效率；同时实现带经验回放和目标网络的深度Q学习智能体，通过调度与路由调整优化动态交通分配；将学习到的潜在表示同时用于贝叶斯校准降维和强化学习状态表示。

Result: 在基准网络上，组合式MLP自编码器相比传统降维方法具有更高样本效率，在固定计算预算下对观测数据拟合更好；基于该框架的控制策略使系统总行程时间相较基线运行最多降低51%。

Conclusion: 共享潜在空间为智能交通系统提供了从仿真器校准到自适应运行控制的统一路径，表明深度学习方法在城市交通规划与管理、尤其大规模网络中具有显著潜力，可缓解传统优化方法的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Urban transportation networks present complex optimization challenges spanning calibration of high-fidelity simulators and real-time operational control. This paper presents a shared latent-space framework that connects simulator calibration and reinforcement learning control through a common learned representation of urban traffic dynamics. First, we develop a combinatorial MLP-autoencoder architecture that learns low-dimensional manifolds linking simulator inputs (origin-destination demand, network parameters) to outputs (travel times, congestion patterns), enabling efficient Bayesian optimization for calibration. This approach demonstrates superior sample efficiency compared to traditional dimension reduction methods, achieving better fit to observational data within fixed computational budgets. Second, we implement a deep Q-learning agent with experience replay and target networks to optimize dynamic traffic assignment through scheduling and routing adjustments. In empirical evaluations on benchmark networks, our approach reduces system-wide travel times by up to 51% compared to baseline operations. The learned latent representation is not only used to reduce the dimensionality of Bayesian calibration, but is also incorporated into the reinforcement learning state representation, allowing the control policy to operate on compressed and calibrated traffic dynamics. This shared latent-space formulation provides a unified pathway from simulator calibration to adaptive operational control within intelligent transportation systems. Our results highlight the transformative potential of deep learning methods in urban mobility planning and management, particularly for large-scale networks where traditional optimization approaches face computational bottlenecks.

</details>


### [252] [RACER: Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Human-AI Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21953)
*Joshua Strong,Emma Sun,Alexander Capstick,Pramit Saha,Cheng Ouyang,J. Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RACER：一种基于角色相对（role-relative）胜任度估计的路由框架，用候选类别角色下的后验预测概率刻画未见专家的实例级胜任度，实现类别重标记不变性并给出贝叶斯对齐的延迟代理与遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "学习何时延迟"要求预测系统判断何时自主决策、何时交由人类专家。群体自适应延迟（population-adaptive deferral）借助少量专家行为上下文集合，把该问题推广到未见专家。现有神经上下文编码器（如 L2D-Pop）可依赖查询，但可能学到与绝对类别坐标绑定的"路由捷径"；Identity-Free Deferral（IFD）通过角色索引的类别级胜任度画像消除了这类捷径，但其估计在类别内恒定，无法刻画实例级专家专长。

Method: 提出 RACER（Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing）这一角色相对框架，从上下文估计未见专家的胜任度：先在每个候选类别角色下估计专家对某查询正确的后验预测概率，再与模型后验结合，得到贝叶斯相关的"专家正确概率"。估计器包含非参数与神经核池化（kernel-pooling）两类，利用候选角色关系、共享聚合与对称汇总，剔除绝对类别身份通道。理论方面证明一致的类别重标记不变性，推导贝叶斯对齐的延迟代理目标，并给出把路由遗憾与分类器误差、胜任度估计误差联系起来的 plug-in 遗憾界。

Result: 在受控合成基准上（含使用模拟专家的 PathMNIST 组织病理学上下文扩展研究），RACER 在隐式子类型依赖下能从更多上下文中获益；在 CIFAR-100 合成实验中，于单独采样的未见专家划分上取得最强综合性能。在放射科医师与人类–AI 胸部 X 光基准（VinDr-CXR、CheXpert）上，RACER 系列在预算扫描式延迟中表现具有竞争力或最优，但校准结果随指标与数据集而变化。

Conclusion: RACER 通过角色相对、排除绝对类别身份的胜任度估计，将群体自适应延迟从类别级提升到实例级，并在理论与实验上验证了类别重标记不变性、贝叶斯对齐的延迟决策及较强的未见专家泛化能力，同时指出校准表现尚不稳定、依赖指标与数据。

Abstract: Learning to defer asks a predictive system when to act autonomously and when to defer to a human expert. Population-adaptive deferral extends this problem to unseen experts using a small context set of expert behavior. Neural context encoders such as L2D-Pop can be query-dependent, but may learn routing shortcuts tied to absolute class coordinates. Identity-Free Deferral (IFD) removes such shortcuts through role-indexed classwise competence profiles, but its estimates are constant within each class and cannot capture instance-level expert specialization. We propose RACER---Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing---a role-relative framework for estimating an unseen expert's competence from context. RACER estimates the posterior-predictive probability that the expert is correct on a query under each candidate class role, then combines these estimates with the model posterior to obtain the Bayes-relevant expert-correctness probability. Nonparametric and neural kernel-pooling estimators use candidate-role relations, shared aggregation, and symmetric summaries, excluding absolute class-identity channels. We prove coherent class-relabelling invariance, derive a Bayes-aligned deferral surrogate, and give a plug-in regret bound relating routing regret to classifier and competence-estimation error. On controlled synthetic benchmarks, including a PathMNIST histopathology context-scaling study with simulated experts, RACER benefits from additional context under hidden subtype dependence and gives the strongest aggregate performance on a separately sampled unseen-expert split in the CIFAR-100 synthetic experiments. On the radiologist and human--AI chest-radiography benchmarks (VinDr-CXR and CheXpert), the RACER family is competitive or best in budget-swept deferral, with calibration results varying across metrics and datasets.

</details>


### [253] [Time series generation with spectrally aligned latent flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21989)
*Camilo Carvajal Reyes,Felipe Tobar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"频谱对齐的潜在流匹配"时间序列生成器，通过在潜在空间中引入基于傅里叶、小波和签名变换的微调损失，解决潜在压缩导致的频谱失配等伪影问题，从而提升合成样本作为训练代理的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在流模型（latent flow models）是高效、低成本的时间序列生成方法，但潜在压缩会引入不必要的伪影，例如合成数据与原始数据集之间存在频谱失配，限制了其作为训练代理（training surrogates）的可用性。仅依赖逐点重建损失无法保证生成信号在动态特性上与真实信号一致。

Method: 作者提出频谱对齐的潜在流时间序列生成器，其核心是训练用于流匹配的潜在空间，使其保留对合成样本适用性至关重要的动态特性。具体做法是引入基于经典信号表示的微调损失，包括傅里叶变换、小波变换与签名变换（signature transforms）。借助这些变换的可解释性，确保合成信号在平滑度、目标频谱内容等相关特征上与真实信号对齐，而非仅依赖逐点重建损失。

Result: 在真实世界的长程单变量与多变量基准数据集上，将所提对齐模型与基础潜在流模型及当前最先进方法进行比较。定量结果表明，所提方法在反映信号真实性的指标与计算效率上均具优势，同时在局部结构上与训练集保持一致。

Conclusion: 基于傅里叶、小波与签名变换的微调损失能够有效缓解潜在压缩带来的频谱失配问题，使频谱对齐的潜在流模型在信号真实性、计算效率和局部结构对齐方面优于基线方法，验证了以可解释信号表示约束潜在空间的可行性。

Abstract: Latent flow models have proven to be a reliable and cost-effective method for time series generation. However, the latent compression induces unwanted artefacts, such as a spectral mismatch with respect to the underlying dataset, thus hindering their use as training surrogates. In this article, we propose a spectrally-aligned latent-flow time series generator, where the latent space for flow matching is trained to preserve dynamical properties that are relevant for the suitability of synthetic samples. We find that incorporating fine-tuning losses based on canonical signal representations such as the Fourier, wavelet and signature transforms helps overcome these issues. The interpretability of these transformations allows us to ensure that the synthetic signals are aligned with the true ones in terms of relevant features, such as smoothness or targeted spectral content, as opposed to relying on pointwise reconstruction losses only. We compare the proposed aligned models against a base latent-flow model and the state of the art over real-world long-range univariate and multivariate benchmark datasets. Our quantitative results validate the superiority of the proposed method in terms of its performance on metrics reflecting signal realness and computational efficiency, while being aligned to the training set with respect to its local structure.

</details>


### [254] [Assessment of Machine Learning-Based Critical Heat Flux Models in the CTF Subchannel Code for Square Rod Bundle Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21995)
*Aidan Furlong,Vinicius de Melo Monteiro,Robert Salko,Juliana Pacheco Duarte,Xu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在CTF子通道程序中评估基于机器学习的临界热流密度（CHF）模型，使用EPRI棒束CHF数据库，比较纯模型与混合残差修正模型（局部/半局部），并与传统经验关系式和查询表对比。结果表明，仅用管束数据训练的ML模型可较好地迁移到方形棒束，局部混合LUT模型综合性能最佳，半局部纯ML模型也具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHF是核热工水力中的关键安全参数，直接影响燃料性能与反应堆安全。传统经验关系式和LUT精度有限，而现有ML-CHF模型多基于圆管数据库开发与评估，其在反应堆相关的棒束几何中的适用性尚不明确，因此需要大规模评估。

Method: 将ML-based CHF模型部署于CTF子通道代码中，采用EPRI棒束CHF数据库进行验证；考虑纯ML模型与混合残差修正模型，并分别采用局部（local）和半局部（semilocal）形式；与Bowring关系式、W-3关系式及2006 Groeneveld LUT进行基准对比。

Result: 管束训练的ML CHF模型总体能较好地迁移至棒束应用，在多数几何和运行工况下优于传统CHF方法；局部混合LUT模型总体性能最强，半局部纯ML模型仍具高度竞争力；相比Bowring、W-3和2006 Groeneveld LUT，即使仅用管束数据训练，也能显著提升棒束CHF预测精度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在生产级子通道分析环境中对ML-based CHF模型在方形棒束中进行大规模评估，证明其从管束到棒束的可迁移性与优越性，支持ML-CHF模型在反应堆热工水力分析中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: The prediction of critical heat flux (CHF), a key safety-related quantity in nuclear thermal hydraulics, remains an important challenge due to its direct relationship with fuel performance and reactor safety. Recent studies have demonstrated that relative to traditional empirical correlations and lookup tables (LUTs), machine learning (ML) methods can substantially improve CHF prediction accuracy. Most ML-based CHF models, however, have been developed and evaluated using tube databases, leaving their applicability to reactor-relevant rod bundle geometries largely unexplored.
  This study evaluates ML-based CHF models deployed within the CTF subchannel code using the Electric Power Research Institute (EPRI) rod bundle CHF database. Both pure and hybrid residual correction models are considered in local and semilocal formulations. The tube-trained ML CHF models generally transferred favorably to rod bundle applications and outperformed traditional CHF methods across most geometries and operating conditions. The local hybrid LUT model produced the strongest overall performance, and the semilocal pure ML model remained highly competitive. Comparison against the Bowring correlation, W-3 correlation, and 2006 Groeneveld LUT demonstrated that substantial improvements in rod bundle CHF prediction are possible even when models are trained exclusively on tube data. These findings provide one of the first large-scale assessments of ML-based CHF models in square rod bundles within a production-level subchannel analysis environment and support their broader application in reactor thermal hydraulic analysis.

</details>


### [255] [Abstention and Noise Filtering: Two Missing Primitives of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22005)
*Richard Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文主张：对注意力值通路加门控能提升语言模型预训练，是因为门控提供了 softmax 注意力所缺乏的两种原语——弃权（abstention）与噪声过滤（noise filtering）；二者随模型规模作用不同，且同时具备时效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究观察到门控值通路可改善语言模型预训练，但对其原因存在分歧。作者试图厘清门控到底提供了什么机制，并解释不同规模下收益来源为何不同。

Method: 在10M至350M参数的匹配模型上进行实验：用每个注意力头可学习的 sink logit 注入 softmax，实现弃权，使注意力头可输出空结果，绕过注意力权重必须归一化为1的限制；在每个 value 上加门控，实现噪声过滤，抑制残差流中叠加特征带来的干扰。比较不同规模、不同原语组合的模型，并向注意力头读取的 value 注入受控干扰以验证门控作用。

Result: 三项发现：第一，弃权收益（相对匹配基线的验证损失下降）随模型增大而下降，噪声过滤收益随规模增大而上升；10M时弃权几乎贡献全部门控增益，350M时过滤贡献大部分增益。第二，每个规模下最佳模型都是同时内置两种原语的模型。第三，受控干扰注入确认门控能去除干扰，并揭示所研究的两种门控形式各有特征性盲点。

Conclusion: 门控带来的预训练增益可分解为弃权与噪声过滤两个互补原语：前者在小型模型更重要，后者在大型模型更重要。同时提供两种原语仅增加可忽略的参数，并与 key-value cache 兼容。

Abstract: Gating the value pathway of attention reportedly improves language model pretraining, and prior studies disagree on why. We argue and provide experimental evidence that such gates supply two different things that softmax attention lacks: abstention and noise filtering. The first is abstention, which allows an attention head to output nothing, bypassing the requirement that attention weights must sum to one. The second is noise filtering, which allows the value pathway of an attention head to suppress interference from superposed features in the residual stream. In our experiments in matched models from 10M to 350M parameters, we supply abstention through a learned per-head sink logit in the softmax and noise filtering through a gate on each value. We report three empirical findings. First, the benefit of abstention, measured as the reduction in validation loss relative to a matched baseline, declines as models grow, whereas the benefit of noise filtering increases with scale. In particular, abstention accounts for nearly all of the gain from gating at 10M and filtering for most of it at 350M. Second, the best model at every scale is the one with both primitives built in. Third, injecting controlled interference into the values a head reads confirms that the gate removes such interference, and reveals that each of the two gate forms we study has a characteristic blind spot. Supplying both primitives adds negligible parameters and remains compatible with the key-value cache.

</details>


### [256] [COMPLEX: A Closed-Form Certified Embedding of Multiparameter Persistence Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22012)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 COMPLEX——一种闭式、免训练的多参数持久性模嵌入方法，首次为多参数特征映射给出双侧失真界，并在 Orbit 与分子图基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参数持久性向量化方法均只具备单侧 Lipschitz 上界，缺乏下界（下确界标尺）。没有下界意味着无法保证特征保真（faithful），也无法据此构建逐预测（per-prediction）的可靠性保证。本文旨在补齐这一缺失的下侧，使保真度可度量。

Method: COMPLEX 是一种闭式、免训练的嵌入：沿固定的近对角网（near-diagonal net）对多参数模做切片，用经过认证的 PLACE/PALACE 地标映射（landmark map）嵌入每条切片条形码，再拼接。在可验证的"见证切片一致性（witnessing-slice coherence）"条件下，单个切片即可给出闭式下界标尺：分离的模在嵌入中仍保持分离。结合标准上界，构成首个双侧失真界。

Result: 在 Orbit5k 上，一致性条件在 100% 的审计对上成立。下界紧致度在实测距离的小因子范围内，但操作上具有局部性：同一特征上 RBF-SVM 达 91%，而 1-NN 仅 78%，说明逐预测认证因结构性原因失效（凡下界标尺由单一坐标见证的地标嵌入皆然）。无学习嵌入、无留出校准，仅用交叉验证的 SVM 头，COMPLEX 在两个 Orbit 基准上均达最优（Orbit5k 91.95%，Orbit100k 92.98%），与 Euler 特征曲面持平或更优，且优于 transformer 与 graphcode。在图上，单一固定配置在全部四个共享分子基准上超过 GRIL，是唯一在 COX2 上超出多数基线 3 个点以上的多参数方法。

Conclusion: COMPLEX 通过闭式下界标尺实现了多参数特征映射的可度量保真度，并证明其无法支持逐预测认证（结构性局限）。闭式选择（地标半径、保持认证的核函数、双滤流集）可进一步提升精度，而梯度式的自适应调整则无增益。

Abstract: Every multiparameter persistence vectorization we know of carries a one-sided Lipschitz upper bound and nothing below it: without a lower gauge there is no sense in which the features are faithful, and no per-prediction guarantee can be built on them. This paper supplies the missing side. COMPLEX is a closed-form, training-free embedding of multiparameter modules -- slice the module along a fixed near-diagonal net, embed each slice barcode by the certified PLACE/PALACE landmark map, concatenate. Under a checkable witnessing-slice coherence condition, holding on 100% of audited pairs on Orbit5k, a single slice carries a closed-form lower gauge: separated modules stay separated in the embedding. With the standard upper bound this gives, to our knowledge, the first two-sided distortion bound for a multiparameter feature map, making faithfulness measurable. Measuring it, we find the floor tight within a small factor of realized distances yet operationally local: an RBF-SVM reaches 91% where 1-NN reaches 78% on the same features. Local per-prediction certification therefore fails for a structural reason common to every landmark embedding whose lower gauge is witnessed by one coordinate. With no learned embedding and no held-out calibration -- only a cross-validated SVM head -- COMPLEX sets the state of the art on both Orbit benchmarks (91.95% on Orbit5k, 92.98% on Orbit100k), level with or above Euler-characteristic surfaces and above transformers and graphcode. On graphs it exceeds GRIL on all four shared molecular benchmarks with one fixed configuration, including the only multiparameter method to clear COX2's majority baseline by more than three points. Closed-form selection -- of the landmark radius, the kernel (certificate-preserving), and the bifiltration set -- buys further accuracy; gradient-shaped adaptation buys none.

</details>


### [257] [$λ$-Controlled GRPO: Turning Flow-Matching Ratio Instability into a Budgeted Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22041)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Meeshawn Marathe,Ramit Pahwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 λ-Controlled GRPO，指出 Flow-GRPO 在多步去噪强化学习中的不稳定源于一个可由采样器高斯转移核精确决定的逐步骤量——路径方差，并据此校准重要性比率与分配梯度预算，从而提升文本渲染与人类偏好对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习被用于将图像生成器与奖励信号对齐，Flow-GRPO 进一步把去噪采样器视为可由奖励反馈优化的随机策略。但多步去噪训练存在特有且系统性的不稳定：策略更新随去噪步骤系统性变化，重要性比率低于 1、离散度增大、裁剪率不同，训练后期可用样本减少。已有工作将这些视为彼此独立的失效模式，并分别使用手工调参的稳定器处理。

Method: 作者证明上述现象来自单一逐步骤量，称为路径方差。该量由采样器的高斯转移核精确决定，并可在训练中低成本估计，从而把不稳定性重新表述为可测量、可预算化的资源。所提 λ-Controlled GRPO 根据预测规律而非噪声经验统计来校准重要性比率行为，并依据预测成本在不同去噪步骤间分配梯度更新力度；控制更新的两个尺度由标准策略选择确定，而非引入自由调参参数。

Result: 在文本到图像模型上、两种奖励设置下——由 OCR 评分的困难目标文本渲染，以及由偏好模型评分的人类偏好匹配——λ-Controlled GRPO 在文本准确率和偏好奖励上均优于最强经验稳定器。同时，它在后期步骤将路径方差保持在预期预算内，而基线正是在此处系统性超出预算。

Conclusion: 该方法使 Flow-GRPO 更新由其自身转移规律校准，而非在 instability 出现后再进行稳定化；将多步去噪 RL 的不稳定统一解释为路径方差并进行预算控制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is increasingly used to align image generators with reward signals, and Flow-GRPO recently extended this paradigm to flow-matching models by treating the denoising sampler as a stochastic policy that can be optimized from reward feedback. Training in this setting is unstable in a way specific to multi-step denoising: the policy update changes systematically across denoising steps, with importance ratios drifting below one, becoming increasingly dispersed, clipping at different rates, and leaving fewer usable samples late in training. Prior work treats these effects as separate failure modes and addresses each with a hand-tuned stabilizer. We show instead that they arise from a single per-step quantity, which we call path variance. This quantity is determined exactly by the sampler's Gaussian transition kernel and can be estimated cheaply during training. This reframes instability as a resource that can be measured and budgeted rather than a collection of symptoms to repair. Our method, $λ$-Controlled GRPO, calibrates importance-ratio behavior from this predicted law rather than from noisy empirical statistics, and allocates gradient effort across denoising steps according to their predicted cost. The two scales governing the update are fixed by standard policy choices rather than introduced as free tuning parameters. On a text-to-image model under two reward settings, rendering difficult target text scored by optical character recognition and matching human preferences scored by a preference model, $λ$-Controlled GRPO improves both text accuracy and preference reward over the strongest empirical stabilizer. It also keeps late-step path variance within its intended budget, precisely where the baseline systematically overshoots. The result is a Flow-GRPO update calibrated by its own transition law rather than stabilized after instability appears.

</details>


### [258] [Available Guardrails: Certifying Selective Prediction across ML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22048)
*Parivesh Priye,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling,Michael Chaykowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究选择性预测器作为安全门时的“可认证可用性”：在有限校准数据下，每个报告单元（工具、策略标签、患者亚组等）能否在目标精度上获得认证。作者用精确二项反演量化可用性，将报告分区选择建模为动态规划，揭示安全、粒度与已服务流量之间的权衡，并提出跨切分选择与族错误预算重分配来提升覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署场景要求对每个报告单元在目标精度下认证可靠性。核心难点不是已颁发证书是否有效，而是有限校准数据能否产生证书；当安全门更严格或报告单元更细时，部分单元会因证据不足而无法认证。

Method: 以经典精确二项反演使可用性可计算；在固定组序下，将报告分区选择形式化为动态规划，刻画安全、粒度与已服务流量之间的权衡。提出在一个规划切分上构造候选分区、在另一个切分上选择；并引入重分配族错误率预算这一保有效性的补充杠杆。评估覆盖三个意图路由数据集、两种架构，以及LLM工具调用、内容审核、病灶分类和推荐任务。

Result: 总体规划前沿显示巨大机会：真相知情规划者相比支持平衡平均覆盖率提升0.157，而朴素估计仅恢复0.005，表明有限数据恢复是核心挑战。跨切分构造与选择可恢复0.060的平均覆盖率提升，方向在60个模型效应中的59个复现。族错误预算重分配在总体量和含噪估计下均带来额外覆盖。相同前沿在多个任务中重现，并呈现预测器特定上限。

Conclusion: 认证可用性是一种可规划的部署资源，决定安全门何时可被认证、以何种粒度认证，以及能覆盖多少流量。

Abstract: A selective predictor acts as a safety gate: it returns an output only when the prediction appears sufficiently trustworthy. Deployments increasingly require this reliability to be certified at a target precision for every reporting unit of interest, such as a tool, policy label, or patient subgroup. The main difficulty is often not whether a granted certificate is valid, but whether finite calibration data can produce one at all. As the gate becomes safer or more fine-grained, some units may receive too little evidence to certify. We make this notion of availability computable through classical exact-binomial inversion and formulate reporting-partition selection, under a fixed group order, as a dynamic program that exposes the trade-off among safety, granularity, and served traffic. The resulting frontier reveals a large population opportunity that finite-sample estimation nearly erases: a truth-informed planner gains $0.157$ mean coverage over support balancing, whereas a naive estimator recovers only $0.005$, making recovery from finite data the central challenge. Constructing candidate partitions on one planning split and selecting among them on another recovers part of this gap, improving mean coverage over support balancing by $0.060$, with the direction reproduced in $59$ of $60$ model effects across three intent-routing datasets and two architectures. A complementary validity-preserving lever, reallocating the familywise error budget across reporting units, recovers additional coverage both with population quantities and noisy estimates. The same frontier recurs, with predictor-specific ceilings, across LLM tool-calling, content moderation, lesion classification, and recommendation. Certified availability is therefore a plannable deployment resource that determines when a safety gate can be certified, at what granularity, and over how much traffic.

</details>


### [259] [Particle Competition and Cooperation for Robust Graph Convolutional Network Learning Under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22053)
*Fabricio Breve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PCC+GCN 混合框架：在 GCN 训练前用粒子竞争与合作（PCC）做基于图的标签精炼，识别可疑标注节点并决定保留、删除或重新分配标签，从而提升 GCN 在标签噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCN 对标签噪声高度敏感，错误监督会沿图结构传播并破坏节点表示学习，因此需要一种有效的标签精炼预处理手段来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 将 PCC（Particle Competition and Cooperation）作为 GCN 前的标签精炼阶段：通过粒子支配动力学识别可疑标注节点，并判定其标签应保留、移除或重新分配。PCC 所用图可用基于特征的 k 近邻边增强，而 GCN 仍在原始图结构与节点特征上训练。

Result: 在 NoisyGL 基准的 10 个图数据集上，针对 Uniform、Pair、Random 传统噪声及实例依赖噪声进行评估，并在 Cora、CiteSeer、PubMed 上做超参数分析。传统噪声下，PCC+GCN 取得最高平均准确率与最佳平均排名，相较基线 GCN 在干净及全部噪声场景平均提升 1.67 个百分点；实例依赖噪声下与最强鲁棒方法相当，但执行时间显著更低，在 10 个数据集中有 8 个为最快的鲁棒方法。

Conclusion: PCC 标签精炼是一种有效且计算高效的数据预处理策略，可提升 GCN 在噪声监督下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) are highly sensitive to label noise, since corrupted supervision can propagate through the graph and degrade learned node representations. This work proposes PCC+GCN, a hybrid framework that uses Particle Competition and Cooperation (PCC) as a graph-based label-refinement stage before GCN training. PCC identifies suspicious labeled nodes through particle domination dynamics and determines whether their labels should be preserved, removed, or reassigned before GCN training. The framework also allows the graph used by PCC to be augmented with feature-based $k$-nearest-neighbor edges, while the GCN itself is trained on the original graph structure and node features. The proposed method was evaluated on ten graph datasets from the NoisyGL benchmark under conventional Uniform, Pair, and Random label noise, as well as under instance-dependent label noise. A detailed hyperparameter analysis was also conducted on Cora, CiteSeer, and PubMed. Under conventional noise, PCC+GCN achieved the highest overall average accuracy and the best average rank among the evaluated methods, with an average gain of $1.67$ percentage points over the baseline GCN across the clean setting and all noisy scenarios. Under instance-dependent noise, PCC+GCN remained competitive with the best-performing robust methods while requiring substantially lower execution time, being the fastest robust method on eight of the ten datasets. The results indicate that PCC-based label refinement provides an effective and computationally efficient preprocessing strategy for improving GCN robustness under noisy supervision.

</details>


### [260] [Benchmarking World Models for Continual Learning on Compositional Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22055)
*Haoyu Zhou,Joe Watson,Anson Lei,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向机器人操作世界模型的组合式持续学习基准，通过沿动作与感知轴分解任务组合来隔离知识复用，发现模块化世界模型比常规持续学习方法更能平衡复用与遗忘，但尚未完全解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想世界模型应能跨任务持续学习，在适应新环境时不遗忘已学知识，并复用先前经验以高效适应，因为物理世界动态常由重复机制构成。然而，现有对“适应”的度量混淆了两类能力：学习未见任务的速度与容量，以及已有知识的复用；新任务通常同时包含新内容与重复内容。因此，需要专门设计基准来隔离并评估知识复用。

Method: 作者提出一个用于机器人操作世界模型的组合式持续学习基准。具体地，为每个任务课程设计组合任务，将序列中已见任务的若干方面重新组合；并沿动作与感知两个轴对组合进行因子分解，以分析不同输入模态如何构成知识复用的瓶颈。评估方面，在典型持续学习方法下测试最先进世界模型，并加入一个模块化世界模型，其动力学主干包含显式可复用组件。

Result: 结果表明，模块化世界模型在知识复用与灾难性遗忘之间取得了优于常规持续学习方法的平衡；但没有一种方法能够完全解决该问题，说明面向“可复用且不遗忘”的持续世界模型仍存在明显研究空间。

Conclusion: 该工作通过组合式任务课程和动作/感知轴分解，提供了可隔离评估知识复用的持续学习基准。模块化是缓解复用与遗忘权衡的有效方向，但尚不足以彻底解决，未来需要构建真正具备复用能力且不遗忘的持续世界模型。项目网站为 https://object814.github.io/Compositional-Continual-Learning/。

Abstract: A desirable property of a world model is the ability to learn continually across tasks, adapting to new environments without forgetting what the agent has already learnt. In particular, the ability to retain and reuse knowledge obtained from prior experiences underpins an agent's ability to efficiently adapt to novel environments, as the dynamics of the physical world can often be described in recurring mechanisms. However, the world model's measure of adaptation entangles two abilities: the speed and capacity to learn unseen tasks, and the reuse of knowledge already acquired, since incoming tasks carry novel content alongside what recurs. In order to isolate knowledge reuse from prior experiences, we propose a compositional continual learning benchmark for world models in robot manipulation. Specifically, we design each task curriculum with compositional tasks that combine aspects of the tasks seen in the sequence. We further factorise this composition along the axes of action and perception to better understand how different input modalities bottleneck knowledge reuse. We evaluate state-of-the-art world models under canonical continual learning methods, alongside a modular world model whose dynamics backbone contains explicitly reusable components. Results show that modularity balances reuse against forgetting better than conventional methods, but none solve the problem fully, leaving clear room for continual world models built to reuse without forgetting. More details are available on our project website: https://object814.github.io/Compositional-Continual-Learning/.

</details>


### [261] [BrainWideBench: Benchmarking large-scale pretraining and across-animal transfer in multi-region neural recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22064)
*Alexandre Andre,Shivashriganesh P. Mahato,Vinam Arora,Keshav Balaji,Divyansha Lachi,Nanda H. Krishna,Jingyun Xiao,Yizi Zhang,Ximeng Mao,Wenrui Ma,Han Yu,International Brain Laboratory,Daniel Birman,Niccolò Bonacchi,Gaelle A. Chapuis,Joana A. Catarino,Felicia Davatolhagh,Mayo Faulkner,Laura Freitas-Silva,Fei Hu,Julia M. Huntenburg,Anup Khanal,Inês Laranjeira,Petrina Lau,Guido T. Meijer,Nathaniel J. Miska,Jean-Paul Noel,Alejandro Pan-Vazquez,Georg Raiser,Cyrille Rossant,Karolina Z. Socha,Anne E. Urai,Miles J. Wells,Steven J. West,Olivier Winter,Blake Richards,Guillaume Lajoie,Cole Hurwitz,Mehdi Azabou,Matthew R. Whiteway,Liam Paninski,Eva L. Dyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BrainWideBench——一个基于 IBL Brainwide Map 数据集（139 只小鼠、276 个脑区）的跨个体多区域神经记录迁移评测基准，包含三个互补任务套件，系统评估预训练方法在下游解码、神经活动预测与解剖结构恢复上的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经记录技术已能跨多动物、多脑区采集数据，引发"能否利用此规模学习可跨任务迁移的通用神经表征"这一问题。但该目标进展受限，原因在于评测协议碎片化，且研究仅聚焦于单一任务领域，缺乏统一、可复现的评测框架。

Method: 构建 BrainWideBench 基准，基于国际脑实验室（IBL）Brainwide Map 数据集，涵盖 139 只小鼠执行感官引导决策任务、覆盖 276 个脑区的神经与行为记录。基准围绕三个互补任务套件组织：①评估表征是否支持行为的下游解码；②预测被掩码的或未来的神经活动；③恢复具生物学意义的解剖组织。据此系统评估多种预训练方法，设置包括下游目标微调与对未见动物的零样本泛化等迁移场景。

Result: 结果确认预训练相较匹配的单会话基线能提升性能；但当前方法在迁移能力上表现出异质性——增益强烈依赖于预训练目标与下游任务之间的一致性。没有单一方法能在三个套件上一致表现优异，且多数方法仅针对其中一部分设计。

Conclusion: 学习能同时在行为、动力学与解剖三个维度泛化的表征仍是一项开放挑战。BrainWideBench 通过提供统一且可复现的评测套件，为衡量面向小鼠大脑通用模型的进展建立了框架。

Abstract: Advances in large-scale neural recording have made it possible to collect data across many animals and distributed brain regions, raising the question of whether this scale can be exploited to learn general-purpose neural representations transferable across diverse downstream tasks. Yet, progress toward this goal has been limited by fragmented evaluation protocols and a narrow focus on individual task domains. Here, we present BrainWideBench, a benchmark for evaluating across-animal transfer on multi-region neural recordings, built on the International Brain Laboratory Brainwide Map dataset of neural and behavioral recordings spanning 276 brain regions from 139 mice performing a sensory-guided decision-making task. The benchmark is organized around three complementary task suites that evaluate whether learned representations support downstream decoding of behavior, can predict masked or future neural activity, and can recover biologically meaningful anatomical organization. With this benchmark, we systematically evaluate pretraining methods across transfer settings, including finetuning on downstream objectives and zero-shot generalization to unseen animals. Our results confirm pretraining improves performance over matched single-session baselines, but we show current methods exhibit heterogeneity in transfer capabilities: gains depend strongly on the alignment between pretraining objectives and downstream tasks. No single approach performs uniformly well across all three suites, and most methods are designed to only address a subset of them. Together, these findings suggest that learning representations that jointly generalize across behavior, dynamics, and anatomy remains an open challenge. By providing a unified and reproducible evaluation suite, BrainWideBench establishes a framework for measuring progress toward general-purpose models of the mouse brain.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Physically Based Rendering in the Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21054)
*Vuk Radovanovic,Vishesh Gupta,Adrien Gruson,Binh-Son Hua*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将基于物理的渲染（PBR）引入到生成模型（VAE）所学习的特征空间中，通过在潜在空间进行光传输模拟，实现物理引导的内容生成，并仅需极少微调即可在预训练潜在空间中精确渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像扩散模型虽然生成能力出色，但控制性较差，不像经典计算机图形管线（如基于物理的渲染）那样可控。作者观察到光传输现象与扩散模型产生的潜在空间值分布之间存在一座"桥梁"，因而希望借助PBR的可控性来弥补扩散模型难以控制的缺陷。

Method: 将基于物理的渲染引入到生成模型中VAE所学习的特征空间，在潜在空间进行光传输模拟；对渲染方程进行修改，并结合可微分渲染器，从而求出一组最优的场景参数；这些参数只需极小的微调即可在预训练的潜在空间中准确渲染。方法仅在单张渲染图像上进行训练。

Result: 仅在单张渲染图像上训练后，方法能够泛化到场景几何变化、光照变化以及相机视角变化等多种情形。

Conclusion: 在潜在空间中进行物理渲染为物理引导的生成式内容创作提供了可行途径，兼顾了经典PBR的可控性与扩散生成模型的强大表现力。

Abstract: Image diffusion models have shown impressive image generation capabilities but are often hard to control, in contrast to classical computer graphics pipelines such as physically based rendering. However, we observe that there is a bridge between light transport phenomena and the distribution of latent space values produced by such models. Thus, we introduce physically based rendering in the feature space learned by the variational autoencoders in generative models, enabling light transport simulation in the latent space. This allows us to leverage physically based rendering techniques to output latent maps for physically guided content generation. We propose modifications to the rendering equation, which, when paired with a differentiable renderer, can yield an optimal set of scene parameters that require only minimal refinement to accurately render into the pretrained latent space. We train our method on a single rendered image, and then demonstrate the generalization of the method to scene geometry changes, lighting changes, and camera view changes.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [RBS-Attention: Radius-Bounded Sparse Prefill for Long-Context Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20971)
*Chuxu Song,Jiuqi Wei,Zhencan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练稀疏预填充方法 RBS-Attention，通过质心基分支与最大键块半径救援分支的双分支选择，缓解均值稀释问题，在保持块稀疏 FlashAttention 执行的同时显著加速长上下文预填充，并维持接近稠密注意力的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理受限于预填充阶段：稠密自注意力需在处理完整提示后才开始生成，成本高昂。现有稀疏块选择虽可降本，但块质心可能掩盖块内少量高相关 token，导致重要块被低估，作者将这一失效模式称为“均值稀释”。

Method: RBS-Attention 是免训练稀疏预填充方法，包含两个互补选择分支：1）质心基分支捕捉平均相关性；2）救援分支利用最大键块半径及其随提示、层、头变化的分布，识别可能被低估的块。对两分支分别独立阈值化并合并掩码，以控制救援块贡献，同时保持常规块稀疏 FlashAttention 执行。

Result: 在 H100 GPU 上，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8、128K 设置下，取得 20.65× 独立预填充注意力加速、11.92× vLLM 预填充注意力加速、5.97× 端到端首 token 时间加速。稠密 Qwen3-32B 上 RULER 总体准确率为 88.65，对比稠密注意力 89.52；并在 LongBench-v2、InfiniteBench、Video-MME 上提供额外质量评估。支撑实验测量实际保留率、在匹配密度下比较选择器，并刻画块大小、阈值与内存行为。

Conclusion: 半径自适应双分支选择是长上下文预填充的有效方法：在显著降低预填充成本的同时，质量接近稠密注意力，并通过多基准与机制实验验证其有效性。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is increasingly limited by prefill, where dense self-attention processes the entire prompt before generation begins. Sparse block selection can reduce this cost, but a block centroid may hide a highly relevant token among many irrelevant ones. We call this failure mode mean dilution and propose RBS-Attention, a training-free sparse-prefill method with two complementary selection branches. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescue branch uses the maximum key-block radius and its prompt-, layer-, and head-dependent distribution to identify blocks at risk of underestimation. Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [264] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出注意力感知路由（AAR），在Mixture-of-Experts模型中用注意力权重滑动窗口的时序与频谱特征增强路由器，同时冻结基座Transformer、仅训练路由参数，从而隔离路由变量并研究路由与注意力的耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE路由通常仅依据当前token的隐藏状态选择与加权专家，上下文信息有限；作者希望引入独立于隐藏状态的上下文摘要来改进路由，并厘清路由变化对注意力机制及不同深度任务表现的影响。

Method: AAR从注意力权重滑动窗口中提取时序与频谱特征，将其作为额外输入增强路由器；训练时冻结基座Transformer，仅更新路由参数，使路由成为唯一变量。进一步通过逐层干预分析路由与注意力之间的耦合，并考察不同层引入AAR的效果差异。

Result: 在OLMoE上，AAR较仅路由SFT基线在GSM8K上提升+3.37个百分点。机制上，第l层路由变化经残差流传播，会放大第l+1层注意力汇（attention sinks），在不直接更新注意力机制的情况下重塑注意力。AAR还减少长程发散生成：错误答案变短，正确答案长度基本不变。此外，AAR具有强深度敏感性：全层 indiscriminate 应用可能损害事实检索，而数学推理增益在更深层引入时仍能保持。

Conclusion: AAR表明注意力携带的路由相关信息可被显式利用，且路由与注意力构成耦合回路。其深度敏感性揭示了事实检索与数学推理在不同网络深度上的张力，使逐层选择性AAR成为探测不同层注意力中路由相关信息的受控探针。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [265] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Causal Latent Revision（CaLR）框架，将推理重构为受约束的潜变量优化，通过专家模型提供的因果拓扑矩阵（CTM）与隐式微分进行梯度引导的“思维修订”，在并行生成中动态自校正中间步骤，兼顾自回归模型的局部贪婪问题与扩散语言模型缺乏严格因果结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归（AR）模型存在局部贪婪（local greediness）问题，而扩散语言模型（DLM）通常缺乏推理所需的严格因果结构。作者希望结合二者优势并克服各自缺陷，提升复杂推理与约束任务中的表现。

Method: 提出 Causal Latent Revision（CaLR），将推理形式化为受约束的潜变量优化问题。具体而言，采用来自专家模型的因果拓扑矩阵（CTM），并结合隐式微分（implicit differentiation），执行梯度引导的“思维修订”（thought revision），以强制逻辑一致性，从而在并行生成过程中实现对中间步骤的动态自校正。

Result: CaLR 在复杂基准上取得了扩散语言模型的 SOTA 性能，超越了强自回归基线，并在如数独（Sudoku）等受约束任务中展现出更优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CaLR 通过因果拓扑约束与潜变量优化，将并行生成与逻辑一致性结合，为扩散语言模型提供动态自校正能力，并在复杂推理和约束任务上验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [266] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将DINOv3 ViT通过三阶段参数高效框架适配到合成孔径声呐自动目标识别，发现仅LoRA适配即显著提升AUPRC（0.300→0.679±0.027，仅训练0.26%权重），后续硬负样本挖掘与SupCon精炼阶段均未超过匹配对照，表明单一高效适配阶段足够，堆叠精炼无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成孔径声呐自动目标识别（SAS ATR）具有重要应用价值，但深度学习受限于目标图像稀缺、背景杂波强以及人工在环评估；需要探索如何将自然图像预训练的ViT高效迁移至水下声学域，并检验复杂精炼策略是否必要。

Method: 提出三阶段参数高效框架：Stage 1冻结ViT主干，使用LoRA适配自然图像预训练与水下声传播之间的域差异；Stage 2采用硬负样本挖掘，针对岩石、沉积物等类人造目标声学混淆物强化决策边界；Stage 3采用监督对比学习（SupCon）分离目标与杂波表征。评估使用海上SAS数据，采用任务级地理划分，在85%测试召回率下比较各方案，并以三个随机种子重复实验。

Result: LoRA是主要效应来源，在同一冻结主干下将AUPRC从0.300提升至0.679±0.027；Rank 4仅训练0.26%权重即达到该结果。Stage 2硬负样本挖掘相对等量随机课程使AUPRC变化-0.0045±0.0119；Stage 3 SupCon相对前一阶段变化+0.0002±0.0096，二者均未超过匹配对照。

Conclusion: 两个精炼阶段均呈零结果/无显著增益，说明硬负样本挖掘发生在编码器已拟合的数据上，且监督阶段已施加大部分目标-杂波几何结构。因此，一个高效适配阶段（LoRA）已足够，堆叠精炼并不必要。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [267] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析注意力图的信息流动拓扑结构（尤其是Forman-Ricci曲率），捕捉幻觉与非幻觉响应在信息瓶颈和上下文共享上的差异，提出一种单遍（single-pass）幻觉检测方法，在多个LLM与基准上优于现有注意力及多响应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法多依赖多次响应采样（multi-response）或仅使用注意力权重，难以刻画注意力图中信息流动的结构性特征。作者希望通过拓扑与曲率视角，识别幻觉响应特有的信息瓶颈，从而更有效地区分幻觉与非幻觉响应。

Method: 1）将注意力图视为信息流网络，计算Forman-Ricci曲率以识别指示信息瓶颈的结构模式；2）提出一种同时捕捉注意力头半局部（semi-local）与全局信息流特征的方法，刻画与幻觉相关的注意力头；3）采用单遍（single-pass）检测流程，无需多次采样。

Result: 在两个幻觉检测基准、多种LLM与不同架构上，所提单遍方法相较现有基于注意力和多响应基线取得一致提升，并具备竞争力的跨架构性能。分析显示：因果生成中token间上下文共享受损与幻觉强相关；幻觉响应在最后一个transformer层尤其表现为过度依赖自注意力、对早期token的上下文检索分散、以及信息过度压缩（over-squashing）。

Conclusion: 注意力图的拓扑/曲率分析可有效刻画幻觉的结构成因，信息流动瓶颈（上下文共享受损、信息过度压缩）是幻觉的重要标志；基于该特征的single-pass方法为幻觉检测提供了高效且具泛化性的新思路。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [268] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究微调如何改变LLM内部表示，并考察这些变化与EAP识别的任务相关组件之间的关系。结果表明EAP组件具有层间功能定位，但其分布与表示变化最大的层不相关；跨任务EAP组件重叠并不能保证正向迁移，反而可能导致性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为将LLM适配到下游任务的主流方法，但其如何重塑模型内部机制仍缺乏理解。作者希望揭示微调对注意力模式、逐层激活等内部表示的影响，并判断这些变化是否对应EAP所识别的、驱动任务表现的关键组件。

Method: 分析微调前后LLM的内部表示变化，包括注意力模式与逐层激活；使用EAP识别任务相关组件（如注意力头和logit级激活）；比较EAP组件的层分布、表示变化显著的层以及不同任务间EAP组件的重叠情况，并评估其与跨任务性能迁移的关系。

Result: EAP识别的组件集中在特定层，表明模型在内部化任务行为时存在一定程度的功能定位；这些组件的层分布与微调中表示变化最显著的层基本不相关；跨任务的EAP组件重叠并不会转化为跨任务性能迁移，尤其当任务性质不同（如分类与生成）时；更具体地，当两个任务的EAP组件高度重叠时，在一个任务上微调可能导致另一个任务性能下降。

Conclusion: 微调带来的表示变化与任务关键组件之间存在解耦：表示变化最大的层不一定是任务相关组件集中的层。EAP组件重叠不足以预测正向迁移，任务性质差异下甚至会出现负迁移或干扰，说明跨任务迁移需综合考虑任务异质性与内部组件冲突。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [269] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TinyCeNN-LM 提出一种“质量门控的后训练转换”框架，用类 CeNN 的细胞循环层替换预训练语言模型中的注意力，仅在表示保真与 NLL 阈值同时通过时接受转换。实验表明可在部分层实现保守转换并降低缓存，但不支持普遍注意力替换或加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预训练语言模型中替换注意力本质上是兼容性问题：候选替代结构可能改变后续层所依赖的表示。因此，需要逐层验证转换是否安全，而非默认所有注意力层都可被统一替换。

Method: 提出 TinyCeNN-LM 质量门控后训练转换框架，采用 CeNN 启发的细胞循环层，具备有界局部处理、紧凑循环记忆、路由、融合以及“接受或回滚”验证机制。研究三种实现：Integrated Memory、MemoryFusion、PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32。其中严格 PDelta3 转换只有在表示与 NLL 指标均通过固定阈值时才接受该层。

Result: 在 SmolLM2-135M 上，第 0–2 层被接受，累计 ΔNLL=+0.01209；第 3 层虽 NLL 可接受，但因表示保真不达标被拒绝。在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 上，全注意力层 3、7、11 被接受，最终 ΔNLL=+0.02073。Integrated Memory 将困惑度控制在 -0.07% 至 +0.93% 变化范围内，总缓存最多减少 6.01%。抽样 200 条下游 sanity check 中，转换后的 Qwen 版本总体准确率为 28.5%–32.0%。

Conclusion: 结果支持保守的、质量门控的结构转换，而非通用注意力替换或加速主张。该框架强调逐层兼容性验证与可回滚机制，适合作为注意力替代方案的安全评估范式，但其通用性和实际加速收益仍有限。

Abstract: Replacing attention in a pretrained language model is a compatibility problem: a plausible substitute may alter representations expected by later layers. TinyCeNN-LM introduces a \emph{quality-gated post-training conversion} framework using CeNN-inspired cellular-recurrent layers with bounded local processing, compact recurrent memory, routing, fusion, and accept-or-rollback validation. Three implementations are studied: Integrated Memory, MemoryFusion, and PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32. Strict PDelta3 conversion accepts a layer only when representation and NLL criteria pass fixed thresholds. On SmolLM2-135M, layers 0-2 are accepted with cumulative $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.01209$, while layer 3 is rejected despite acceptable NLL because representation fidelity fails. On Qwen3.5-0.8B, full-attention layers 3, 7, and 11 are accepted with final $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.02073$. Integrated Memory keeps perplexity within $-0.07\%$ to $+0.93\%$ while reducing total cache by up to $6.01\%$. A sampled 200-item downstream sanity check gives $28.5\%$--$32.0\%$ overall accuracy for converted Qwen releases. The results support conservative, quality-gated structural conversion rather than universal attention replacement or speedup.

</details>


### [270] [Clinician-Grounded Quality Assurance for AI-Assisted Psychiatric Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21149)
*King Shi,Amanda Li,Jonathan Ivey,Synthia Qia Wang,Guan Gui,Hyunseo Kim,Peter Zandi,Jason Straub,Jacob Taylor,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 InterviewPlayground：基于记忆增强患者模拟器的临床医生导向评估平台，用于开放式 AI 精神科问诊。试点中 LLM 问诊者比临床医生回收更多临床相关条目（88.0% vs 38.9%），但产生更多无访谈依据的临床推断（56.8% vs 27.8%），且更少刻画安全顾虑（33.3% vs 66.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康系统在部署 AI 辅助精神科问诊前，需要可常规化、符合临床质量标准的评估手段；同时临床医生问诊风格各异，评估须支持跨访谈方式比较、降低医生负担，并测量与临床部署相关的性能。

Method: 构建以临床医生为基准的评估平台 InterviewPlayground，核心为记忆增强的患者模拟器，用于开放式 AI 访谈；结合专家撰写的病例 vignettes 创建交互式患者，搭建模拟问诊平台，并设计适用于问诊的评估模态；以 6 名临床医生进行 25 分钟试点评估，并与基于 GPT 的 LLM 问诊者比较。

Result: 在试点评估中，LLM 问诊者回收了更多嵌入病例 vignettes 的临床相关条目（88.0% vs. 38.9%），但产生更多并非基于访谈的临床推断（56.8% vs. 27.8%），并且对已识别的安全顾虑进行刻画的比例更低（33.3% vs. 66.7%）。

Conclusion: 该平台为 AI 开放式精神科问诊提供了可部署的质量保证评估路径，表明 LLM 在信息覆盖上具优势，但在推断依据与安全顾虑刻画方面仍需临床监督与针对性评估。

Abstract: Before patients can use AI-assisted psychiatric intake systems, health systems need practical ways to routinely evaluate these tools against their clinical standards for quality assurance. Because clinicians may use different intake styles, evaluation for this task must (1) support comparison across interviewing approaches, (2) minimize clinician burden, and (3) measure clinically relevant performance for health systems deploying these technologies. We present a clinician-grounded evaluation platform built around a memory-augmented patient simulator for open-ended AI interviewing, InterviewPlayground. We created interactive patients using InterviewPlayground with our expert-authored vignettes, constructed a simulated intake platform for the interviews, and designed evaluation modalities relevant to intake.
